    <script id="mbzCompanyDetailMobileAssetGate"
            type="text/javascript"
            data-mobile-overview-src="/type/common/js/company-detail-mobile-overview.js?ver=20261010195546&amp;mbzmobile=20260930-h6ih-persistent-swipe-cue">
        (function () {
            if (window.__MBZ_COMPANY_DETAIL_MOBILE_ASSET_GATE_BOUND === true) return;
            window.__MBZ_COMPANY_DETAIL_MOBILE_ASSET_GATE_BOUND = true;

            var query = '(max-width: 767.98px)';
            var media = window.matchMedia ? window.matchMedia(query) : null;
            var gate = document.currentScript;
            var loaded = false;

            function ensureMobileAssets() {
                if (loaded) return;
                loaded = true;

                ['mbzCompanyDetailMobileOverviewCss', 'mbzCompanyDetailMobileFinalCss'].forEach(function (id) {
                    var link = document.getElementById(id);
                    if (!link || link.getAttribute('href')) return;
                    var href = link.getAttribute('data-href');
                    if (href) link.setAttribute('href', href);
                });

                if (!gate || document.getElementById('mbzCompanyDetailMobileOverviewRuntime')) return;
                var src = gate.getAttribute('data-mobile-overview-src');
                if (!src) return;

                var runtime = document.createElement('script');
                runtime.id = 'mbzCompanyDetailMobileOverviewRuntime';
                runtime.type = 'text/javascript';
                runtime.src = src;
                runtime.async = false;
                document.head.appendChild(runtime);
            }

            function isMobileNow() {
                return media ? media.matches : document.documentElement.clientWidth <= 767;
            }

            if (isMobileNow()) ensureMobileAssets();

            if (media) {
                var onChange = function (event) {
                    if (event.matches) ensureMobileAssets();
                };
                if (typeof media.addEventListener === 'function') media.addEventListener('change', onChange);
                else if (typeof media.addListener === 'function') media.addListener(onChange);
            }
        }());
    </script>

<script>
	var totVistCnt = 0;
	
	/* 유사후보/이웃업체 목록 이동 함수는 company_detail.js 공용 함수 사용 */

	var gBadgeSelectedArr = [];
	function getSearchBadgeId(className) {
		var badgeIds = "";

		$('.'+className).each(function(index,item) {
			if($(this).hasClass('active')) {
				var badgeId = $(this).data("id");

				if (badgeIds.indexOf(badgeId) < 0)
					if (nvl(badgeIds) == "") badgeIds = badgeId;
					else badgeIds += "," + badgeId;

				gBadgeSelectedArr.push($(this).prop('outerHTML').replace('badgeBtn',''));
			}
		});
		//console.log("["+className+"]gBadgeSelectedArr="+gBadgeSelectedArr);
		return badgeIds;
	}

	var placeholderText = [];

	$(function(){
		$(document).on('click', '.tabBtn', function(){
			var val = $(this).data('seq');
			$("#sendForm [name=tabIdx]").val(val);
			$(".tabBtn").removeClass('active');
			$("#tabBtn"+val).addClass('active');
			$(".ibContArea").hide();
			$("#tabcont"+val).removeClass('hide');
			$("#tabcont"+val).show();
		});

		$(document).on('click', '.contactTabBtn', function(){
			var val = $(this).data('seq');
			$("#sendForm [name=tabIdx]").val(val);
			$(".contactTabBtn").removeClass('active');
			$("#contactTabBtn"+val).addClass('active');
			$(".contactIbContArea").hide();
			$("#contactTabcont"+val).removeClass('hide');
			$("#contactTabcont"+val).show();
		});

		$(document).on('click', '.ceoEtcTabBtn', function(){
			var val = $(this).data('seq');
			$("#sendForm [name=tabIdx]").val(val);
			$(".ceoEtcTabBtn").removeClass('active');
			$("#ceoEtcTabBtn"+val).addClass('active');
			$(".ceoEtcIbContArea").hide();
			$("#ceoEtcTabcont"+val).removeClass('hide');
			$("#ceoEtcTabcont"+val).show();
		});

		$(document).on('click', '.badgeBtn', function(){
			var val = $(this).data('seq');
			if($(this).hasClass('active'))
				$("#badgeBtn"+val).removeClass('active');
			else
				$("#badgeBtn"+val).addClass('active');

			var badgeActCnt = 0;
			$('.badgeBtn').each(function(index,item){
				if($(this).hasClass('active'))
					badgeActCnt++;
			});

			if(badgeActCnt!=0){
				$("#badgeBtn0").removeClass('hide');
				$("#badgeBtn0").show();
			}else
				$("#badgeBtn0").hide();
		});

		$(document).on('click', '#badgeBtn0', function() {
			$("#listBadge").val(getSearchBadgeId('badgeBtn'));
			goBadgeSearchList($("#listBadge").val());
		});

		function goBadgeSearchList(selectBadge) {
			$(".loading-wrap-02").show();
			if (selectBadge.includes(",")) {
				top.location.href = "/배지로기업조회/" + selectBadge;
			} else {
				fn_alertModalOpen("배지를 2개 이상 선택해 주세요");
				$(".loading-wrap-02").hide();
			}
		}

		$(document).ready(function() {
			$(".visit-link").click(function(e){
				var id = $(this).attr("data-bno");
				$.get("/updateHomepageVisitorCount", {id: id}, function(){
				});
			});

			fn_lnb_select();

			/*앵커공지*/
			//$("#myAlert").slideUp();
			//$("#myAlert").slideDown();
			/* $("#linkOpen").click(function(){
						$("#myAlert").slideDown();
						return false;
				});

				$("#linkClose").click(function(){
						$("#myAlert").slideUp();
						return false;
				}); */

			// 10초 후 자동으로 닫힘
				/*  setTimeout(function(){
					//$("#myAlert").slideUp();
				}, 10000); */

			/* 기업별 차트 원천이 null·빈 문자열·깨진 JSON이어도 페이지 전체 스크립트를 중단하지 않는다. */
			function mbzSafeArrayPayload(value) {
				if (Array.isArray(value)) return value;
				if (value === null || typeof value === 'undefined' || value === '') return [];
				try {
					var parsed = JSON.parse(String(value));
					return Array.isArray(parsed) ? parsed : [];
				} catch (e) {
					console.warn('[CompanyDetail] chart payload skipped');
					return [];
				}
			}
			function mbzSafeCount(value) {
				var numberValue = Number(value);
				return Number.isFinite(numberValue) && numberValue >= 0 ? numberValue : 0;
			}

			var mbzSalesChartValues = mbzSafeArrayPayload(null);
			var mbzSalesChartColors = mbzSafeArrayPayload(null);
			if (document.getElementById('chartContainer')
					&& typeof doSlsAmtMiniChart1 === 'function'
					&& mbzSalesChartValues.length > 0) {
				try {
					doSlsAmtMiniChart1('chartContainer', mbzSalesChartValues, mbzSalesChartColors);
				} catch (e) {
					console.warn('[CompanyDetail] mini chart render skipped');
				}
			}

			if (typeof doAnimateNumber === 'function') {
				doAnimateNumber('totalVisitCount', mbzSafeCount(7), 200);
				doAnimateNumber('homepageVisitCount', mbzSafeCount(0), 200);
				doAnimateNumber('favoCount', mbzSafeCount(0), 200);
			}
		});
	});
	</script>

    <style>
    .new-alarm {
	    display: inline-block;
	    background-color: #ffcc00;
	    color: #fff;
	    padding: 3px 6px;
	    border-radius: 3px;
	    font-weight: bold;
	    margin-right: 5px;
	    font-size: 0.8em;
	    vertical-align: middle;
	}

	.dropdown-item {
	    display: flex;
	    align-items: center;
	}

	/* R5W6: guest company-detail header typography is the common baseline.
	 * Keep every page/auth state on the same notokr family and compact scale. */
	header > .util > li.user > a,
	header > .util > li.user > a > span {
	    font-family: "notokr", "Malgun Gothic", sans-serif !important;
	    font-weight: 500 !important;
	    font-size: .64rem !important;
	    line-height: 1.15;
	}

	/* R115: the My-page dropdown used .gm (GmarketSans). Because the menu is
	 * hidden initially, browsers can defer that webfont until the first open,
	 * causing a visible font swap. Reuse the already-rendered header notokr
	 * family so the first dropdown paint is final. */
	header > .util > li.user.portfolio .dropdown-menu,
	header > .util > li.user.portfolio .dropdown-menu .dropdown-item {
	    font-family: "notokr", "Malgun Gothic", sans-serif !important;
	    font-weight: 500 !important;
	    font-synthesis: none;
	}

	/* Company-detail mobile header geometry is the common baseline. */
	@media (max-width: 767.98px) {
	    header {
	        width: 100% !important;
	        max-width: 100% !important;
	        min-width: 0 !important;
	        padding-right: 10px !important;
	        padding-left: 8px !important;
	        box-sizing: border-box !important;
	        overflow: visible !important;
	    }

	    header h1,
	    header .mbz-site-logo {
	        flex: 0 1 122px !important;
	        width: 122px !important;
	        min-width: 96px !important;
	        max-width: 122px !important;
	        margin-right: auto !important;
	        margin-left: 38px !important;
	    }

	    header > .util {
	        display: flex !important;
	        flex: 0 0 auto !important;
	        min-width: 0 !important;
	        margin-left: 4px !important;
	        gap: 0 !important;
	        padding-right: 0 !important;
	        overflow: visible !important;
	    }

	    header > .util > li.user {
	        width: 42px !important;
	        min-width: 42px !important;
	        margin-left: 0 !important;
	        flex: 0 0 42px !important;
	    }
	}

	@media (max-width: 480px) {
	    header > .util > li.user > a,
	    header > .util > li.user > a > span {
	        font-size: .54rem !important;
	        line-height: 1.05 !important;
	        letter-spacing: -.035em !important;
	        white-space: nowrap !important;
	        word-break: keep-all !important;
	    }
	}

	@media (max-width: 420px) {
	    header h1,
	    header .mbz-site-logo {
	        flex-basis: 108px !important;
	        width: 108px !important;
	        max-width: 108px !important;
	        margin-left: 32px !important;
	    }

	    header > .util > li.user {
	        flex-basis: 38px !important;
	        width: 38px !important;
	        min-width: 38px !important;
	    }
	}

	/* =========================================================
	 * R100: MarketBZ LIVE broadcast signal - subtle wider spread
	 * - 기존 로고 이미지는 그대로 유지한다.
	 * - 작은 core + 고정된 3단 arc를 순차 점등해 ")))" 송신감을 만든다.
	 * - arc 자체를 이동시키지 않아 링/고리 투척처럼 보이지 않는다.
	 * - R98의 모양/리듬은 유지하고 arc 우측 도달폭만 소폭 넓혀 가시성을 보강한다.
	 * - 하단의 "실시간"은 짧고 선명한 보조 라벨로 유지한다.
	 * ========================================================= */
	header .mbz-site-logo {
	    overflow: visible !important;
	}

	header .mbz-site-logo > a {
	    position: relative;
	    overflow: visible !important;
	}

	header .mbz-site-logo .mbz-live-signal {
	    position: absolute;
	    z-index: 8;
	    top: -5px;
	    right: -32px;
	    width: 32px;
	    height: 22px;
	    pointer-events: none;
	    overflow: visible;
	}

	header .mbz-site-logo .mbz-live-core {
	    position: absolute;
	    left: 0;
	    top: 4px;
	    width: 6px;
	    height: 6px;
	    border-radius: 50%;
	    background: #ff6200;
	    box-shadow:
	        0 0 0 1px rgba(255, 98, 0, .16),
	        0 0 7px rgba(255, 98, 0, .52);
	    transform-origin: center;
	    animation: mbz-live-core-r99 1.66s ease-in-out infinite;
	}

	/* 우측 반원만 보이는 고정 arc. 위치 이동 없이 순차 점등한다. */
	header .mbz-site-logo .mbz-live-wave {
	    position: absolute;
	    box-sizing: border-box;
	    border-radius: 50%;
	    border-style: solid;
	    border-color: transparent;
	    border-right-color: #ff6200;
	    opacity: 0;
	    transform-origin: center;
	    animation: mbz-live-wave-r99 1.66s cubic-bezier(.18, .72, .25, 1) infinite;
	}

	header .mbz-site-logo .mbz-live-wave-1 {
	    left: -1px;
	    top: 1px;
	    width: 13px;
	    height: 13px;
	    border-width: 1.6px;
	    border-right-color: rgba(255, 98, 0, .98);
	    animation-delay: 0s;
	}

	header .mbz-site-logo .mbz-live-wave-2 {
	    left: -3px;
	    top: -1.5px;
	    width: 19px;
	    height: 19px;
	    border-width: 1.55px;
	    border-right-color: rgba(255, 98, 0, .78);
	    animation-delay: .13s;
	}

	header .mbz-site-logo .mbz-live-wave-3 {
	    left: -5px;
	    top: -4px;
	    width: 25px;
	    height: 25px;
	    border-width: 1.45px;
	    border-right-color: rgba(255, 98, 0, .58);
	    animation-delay: .26s;
	}

	header .mbz-site-logo .mbz-live-label {
	    position: absolute;
	    left: 1px;
	    top: 14px;
	    min-width: 24px;
	    color: #e95600;
	    font-family: "notokr", "Malgun Gothic", sans-serif;
	    font-size: 7px;
	    font-weight: 800;
	    line-height: 1;
	    letter-spacing: -.08em;
	    white-space: nowrap;
	    opacity: .78;
	    text-shadow: 0 0 4px rgba(255, 98, 0, .10);
	    animation: mbz-live-label-r99 1.66s ease-in-out infinite;
	}

	@keyframes mbz-live-wave-r99 {
	    0%, 7% {
	        opacity: 0;
	        transform: scale(.88);
	    }
	    15% {
	        opacity: 1;
	        transform: scale(.94);
	    }
	    29% {
	        opacity: .88;
	        transform: scale(1);
	    }
	    45% {
	        opacity: .32;
	        transform: scale(1.035);
	    }
	    55%, 100% {
	        opacity: 0;
	        transform: scale(1.04);
	    }
	}

	@keyframes mbz-live-core-r99 {
	    0%, 100% {
	        transform: scale(.96);
	        filter: brightness(1);
	    }
	    10% {
	        transform: scale(1.22);
	        filter: brightness(1.12);
	    }
	    22% {
	        transform: scale(1);
	        filter: brightness(1.03);
	    }
	    35% {
	        transform: scale(1.10);
	        filter: brightness(1.07);
	    }
	    48%, 78% {
	        transform: scale(.96);
	        filter: brightness(1);
	    }
	}

	@keyframes mbz-live-label-r99 {
	    0%, 100% { opacity: .72; }
	    16%, 36% { opacity: 1; }
	    54%, 78% { opacity: .76; }
	}

	body.darkmode header .mbz-site-logo .mbz-live-core {
	    background: #ff8a1f;
	    box-shadow:
	        0 0 0 1px rgba(255, 170, 96, .20),
	        0 0 8px rgba(255, 138, 31, .62);
	}

	body.darkmode header .mbz-site-logo .mbz-live-wave-1 {
	    border-right-color: rgba(255, 174, 95, 1);
	}

	body.darkmode header .mbz-site-logo .mbz-live-wave-2 {

	}

	body.darkmode header .mbz-site-logo .mbz-live-wave-3 {

	}

	body.darkmode header .mbz-site-logo .mbz-live-label {
	    color: #ffad67;
	    opacity: .88;
	}

	@media (max-width: 767.98px) {
	    header .mbz-site-logo .mbz-live-signal {
	        top: -4px;
	        right: -26px;
	        width: 27px;
	        height: 20px;
	    }

	    header .mbz-site-logo .mbz-live-core {
	        top: 4px;

	        height: 5px;
	    }

	    header .mbz-site-logo .mbz-live-wave-1 {

	        left: -3px;
	        top: -1px;
	        width: 17px;
	        height: 17px;
	        border-width: 1.4px;
	    }

	    /* 모바일은 공간을 위해 2단 신호만 노출한다. */
	    header .mbz-site-logo .mbz-live-wave-3 {
	        display: none;
	    }

	    header .mbz-site-logo .mbz-live-label {

	    }

	@media (max-width: 360px) {

	        right: -22px;
	    }

	    header .mbz-site-logo .mbz-live-label {
	        display: none;
	    }
	}

	@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
	    header .mbz-site-logo .mbz-live-core,
	    header .mbz-site-logo .mbz-live-wave,
	    header .mbz-site-logo .mbz-live-label {
	        animation: none !important;
	    }

	    header .mbz-site-logo .mbz-live-wave-1 { opacity: .88; transform: none; }
	    header .mbz-site-logo .mbz-live-wave-2 { opacity: .62; transform: none; }
	    header .mbz-site-logo .mbz-live-wave-3 { opacity: .42; transform: none; }
	    header .mbz-site-logo .mbz-live-label { opacity: .82; }
	}

	/* R5W20/R114: desktop header utility density.
	 * Preserve the existing icon assets and sizes; adjust spacing only.
	 * Mobile geometry/typography above stays frozen. */
	@media (min-width: 1025px) and (max-width: 1530px) {
	    /* R115: keep the desktop utility-menu silhouette instead of collapsing
	     * every item to the old 36px tablet width. Icons/assets stay unchanged. */
	    header > .util {
	        column-gap: .28rem !important;
	        padding-right: .45rem !important;
	    }

	    header > .util > li.user {
	        width: 3.35rem !important;
	        min-width: 3.35rem !important;
	        margin-left: 0 !important;
	        flex: 0 0 3.35rem !important;
	    }

	    /* The service-intro label is the longest top utility label.
	     * Give only this item a little more breathing room. */
	    header > .util > li.user.company_intro {
	        width: 4.15rem !important;
	        min-width: 4.15rem !important;
	        flex-basis: 4.15rem !important;
	    }

	    header > .util > li.user > a {
	        gap: .22rem !important;
	    }

	    header > .util > li.user > a,
	    header > .util > li.user > a > span {
	        font-size: .68rem !important;
	        line-height: 1.05 !important;
	    }

	    header > .util > li.user > a::before {
	        width: 2.1rem !important;
	        height: 2.1rem !important;
	    }

	    /* Restore some desktop logo weight when the viewport is narrowed by
	     * DevTools or split-screen. The logo asset itself is unchanged. */
	    header .mbz-site-logo {
	        width: 11.25em !important;
	        min-width: 11.25em !important;
	    }
	}

	@media (min-width: 1531px) {
	    /* R116: keep the full desktop header, but tighten the utility cluster
	     * slightly so the four icon labels read as one compact group. */
	    header > .util {
	        column-gap: .2rem !important;
	        padding-right: .4rem !important;
	    }

	    header > .util > li.user {
	        width: 4.8rem !important;
	        min-width: 4.8rem !important;
	        margin-left: 0 !important;
	        flex: 0 0 4.8rem !important;
	    }

	    header > .util > li.user.company_intro {

	    }

	    }

	    header > .util > li.user > a,
	    header > .util > li.user > a > span {
	        font-size: .74rem !important;
	        line-height: 1.08 !important;
	    }

	    header > .util > li.user > a::before {
	        width: 2.4rem !important;
	        height: 2.4rem !important;
	    }
	}

<div id="allPopAnchor" class="alert alert-info collapse all-pop-anchor">
    <ul>
        <li id="popItem-20260508" data-expiration-date="2026-05-08">
            * 현재 접속 사용자 증가로 인해 서버가 일시적으로 원활하지 않을 수 있습니다. 서비스 이용에 불편을 드려 대단히 죄송합니다. - <em class="font8em orange">마켓비즈</em>
            <a href="#" class="orange strong close allClosePopAnchor" title="닫기">x</a>
        </li>
    </ul>
</div>
<style>
    /* 긴급공지용 스타일 */
    .all-pop-anchor {
        display: none; /* 기본적으로 숨김 */
        /* position: relative; */
        background-color: #d9edf7; /* 정보 배경 색상 */
        padding: 5px;
        border: 1px solid #bce8f1; /* 테두리 색상 */
        border-radius: 4px; /* 모서리 둥글게 */
    }
</style>

<script>
$(document).ready(function() {
	$('.allClosePopAnchor').click(function() {
        // 해당 li 항목의 ID를 가져옵니다.
        var itemId = $(this).closest('li').attr('id'); // 가장 가까운 li의 ID를 가져옵니다.
        setCookie(itemId, 'true', 1); // 해당 항목에 대한 쿠키 설정
        $('#' + itemId).slideUp(1000, function() {
            // 슬라이드 업 후 남아있는 보이는 li 항목이 없는지 확인
            if ($('li[id^="popItem"]:visible').length === 0) {
                // 모든 항목이 숨겨졌으면 전체 팝업도 숨김
                $('#allPopAnchor').slideUp(500);
            }
        }); // 해당 항목 슬라이드 업
        return false; // 기본 이벤트 방지
    });

    $('.popup-notice-close-week').click(function() {
        // 해당 li 항목의 ID를 가져옵니다.
        var itemId = $(this).closest('li').attr('id'); // 가장 가까운 li의 ID를 가져옵니다.
        setCookie(itemId, 'true', 7); // 일주일 동안 열리지 않도록 쿠키 설정
        $('#popupNotice').fadeOut(700); // 팝업 닫기
        return false; // 기본 이벤트 방지
    });

    var today = new Date();
    today.setHours(0,0,0,0); // 오늘 날짜의 자정으로 설정
    
    var urgentNoticeVisible = false;
    var popupNoticeVisible = false;

    $('li[id^="popItem"]').each(function() {
        var itemId = $(this).closest('li').attr('id'); // 가장 가까운 li의 ID를 가져옵니다.
        var expirationDate = new Date($(this).data('expiration-date'));

        if (getCookie(itemId) === 'true' || expirationDate < today) {
            $(this).hide(); // 만료되었거나 쿠키가 설정된 경우 숨김
        }else{
        	if ($(this).closest('div').attr('id') === 'allPopAnchor') {
                urgentNoticeVisible = true;
            } else if ($(this).closest('div').attr('id') === 'popupNotice') {
                popupNoticeVisible = true;
            }
        }
    });

    if (urgentNoticeVisible) {
        $('#allPopAnchor').slideDown(500);
    } else {
        $('#allPopAnchor').hide();
    }

    if (popupNoticeVisible) {
        $('#popupNotice').fadeIn(900);
    } else {
        $('#popupNotice').hide();
    }

    // 팝업 닫기 버튼 클릭 시
    $('.popup-notice-close').click(function() {
        $('#popupNotice').fadeOut(900);
    });
});
</script>

    const root = s ? s.closest('section') : null;
    const START_OFFSET = root ? (parseInt(root.getAttribute('data-start-offset') || '0', 10) || 0) : 0;
    if (!root || root.dataset.init === '1') return;
    root.dataset.init = '1';

    const $ = (sel) => root.querySelector(sel);

    const RAW_TL = "[{\"id\":7,\"title\":\"Pro\u00B7Pro Plus \uAD6C\uB9E4 \uC774\uBCA4\uD2B8\",\"subtitle\":\"\uC720\uB8CC \uC774\uC0C1\uC9D5\uD6C4 \uBAA8\uB2C8\uD130\uB9C1 \uC54C\uB9BC \u00B7 \uC778\uC99D \uD61C\uD0DD\",\"subtitle\":\"\uBA85\uD568 \uC778\uC99D \uD6C4 \uB300\uC0C1 \uC870\uD68C \uAD6C\uB3C5\uC0C1\uD488 \uAE30\uC5C5\uD68C\uC6D0\uAC00\",\"href\":\"\/goodsPlan#mbzBusinessMemberBenefit\",\"startDate\":null,\"endDate\":null,\"startTime\":null,\"endTime\":null,\"fixed\":false,\"priority\":108,\"theme\":\"business\"},{\"id\":1,\"title\":\"\uC804\uC790\uACF5\uC2DC \uD604\uD669 \uC624\uD508\",\"subtitle\":\"\uC8FC\uC694\uACF5\uC2DC\u00B7HOT\uACF5\uC2DC\uB97C \uD55C\uB208\uC5D0\",\"href\":\"onclick='return MBZCommonPopup.openDisclosurePayService();'\",\"startDate\":null,\"endDate\":null,\"startTime\":null,\"endTime\":null,\"fixed\":false,\"priority\":100,\"theme\":\"disclosure\"},{\"id\":2,\"title\":\"\uACF5\uC2DC\uD0D0\uC0C9 \uC624\uD508\",\"subtitle\":\"\uAE30\uC5C5 \uC774\uBCA4\uD2B8 \uBE60\uB978 \uD0D0\uC0C9\",\"href\":\"onclick='return MBZCommonPopup.openDisclosurePayService();'\",\"startDate\":null,\"endDate\":null,\"startTime\":null,\"endTime\":null,\"fixed\":false,\"priority\":96,\"theme\":\"disclosure\"},{\"id\":4,\"title\":\"\uC774\uC0C1\uC9D5\uD6C4 \uBAA8\uB2C8\uD130\uB9C1\",\"subtitle\":\"\uACF5\uC2DC\u00B7\uAE30\uC5C5\uC815\uBCF4 \uBCC0\uD654 \uC2E0\uD638\uC640 \uADFC\uAC70 \uD655\uC778\",\"href\":\"\/riskSignalStatus\",\"startDate\":null,\"endDate\":null,\"startTime\":null,\"endTime\":null,\"fixed\":false,\"priority\":92,\"theme\":\"risk\"},{\"id\":5,\"title\":\"\uB9C8\uCF13\uBE44\uC988 \uBE14\uB85C\uADF8\",\"subtitle\":\"\uAE30\uC5C5\uC815\uBCF4 \uB9DB\uC9D1 \uB9C8\uCF13\uBE44\uC988\",\"href\":\"https:\/\/blog.naver.com\/marketbz\",\"startDate\":null,\"endDate\":null,\"startTime\":null,\"endTime\":null,\"fixed\":false,\"priority\":80,\"theme\":\"naver\"},{\"id\":6,\"title\":\"\uAE30\uC5C5\uC815\uBCF4 Live\",\"subtitle\":\"\uC6D0\uCC9C\uBCC4 \uCD5C\uC2E0 \uAC31\uC2E0 \uAE30\uC5C5\uC815\uBCF4 \uD655\uC778\",\"href\":\"\/\uC5C5\uCCB4\uBA85\uC73C\uB85C\uAE30\uC5C5\uC870\uD68C\",\"startDate\":null,\"endDate\":null,\"startTime\":null,\"endTime\":null,\"fixed\":false,\"priority\":60,\"theme\":\"ocean\"}]";
    const RAW =
      (typeof INJECT !== 'undefined' && INJECT) ||
      (typeof window !== 'undefined' && typeof window.INJECT !== 'undefined' && window.INJECT) ||
      (typeof ADS_JSON !== 'undefined' && ADS_JSON) ||
      RAW_TL;

    const VERIFIED_BUSINESS_MEMBER = false;

    let allAds = [];
    try {
      allAds = Array.isArray(RAW) ? RAW : JSON.parse(RAW || '[]');
      if (!Array.isArray(allAds)) allAds = [];
      if (VERIFIED_BUSINESS_MEMBER) {
        allAds = allAds.filter(function(ad) {
          return String(ad && ad.theme || '').toLowerCase() !== 'business';
        });
      }
    } catch(e) {
      allAds = [];
    }

    function nowKST() {
      const n = new Date();
      const utc = n.getTime() + n.getTimezoneOffset() * 60000;
      return new Date(utc + 9 * 3600 * 1000);
    }

    function parseHM(hm) {
      if (!hm) return null;
      const m = String(hm).match(/^(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})(?::(\d{2}))?$/);
      if (!m) return null;
      return (+m[1]) * 60 + (+m[2]);
    }

    function parseYMD(s) {
      if (!s) return null;
      const m = String(s).match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})/);
      if (!m) return null;
      return { y:+m[1], m:+m[2]-1, d:+m[3] };
    }

    function inDateRange(ad, now) {
      const s = parseYMD(ad.startDate);
      const e = parseYMD(ad.endDate);
      if (!s && !e) return true;

      const d = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), now.getDate());

      if (s) {
        const sd = new Date(s.y, s.m, s.d);
        if (d < sd) return false;
      }

      if (e) {
        const ed = new Date(e.y, e.m, e.d);
        if (d > ed) return false;
      }

      return true;
    }

    function inTimeRange(ad, now) {
      const minutes = now.getHours() * 60 + now.getMinutes();
      const s = parseHM(ad.startTime);
      const e = parseHM(ad.endTime);

      if (s == null && e == null) return true;

      if (s != null && e != null) {
        return e >= s ? (minutes >= s && minutes <= e) : (minutes >= s || minutes <= e);
      }

      if (s != null) return minutes >= s;
      if (e != null) return minutes <= e;

      return true;
    }

    function isActive(ad, now) {
      return inDateRange(ad, now) && inTimeRange(ad, now);
    }

    const wrap = $('.mbz-ad-wrap');
    const link = $('.mbz-ad-link');
    const textBox = $('.mbz-ad-text');
    const title = $('.mbz-ad-title');
    const subtitle = $('.mbz-ad-subtitle');
    const dotsWrap = $('.mbz-ad-dots');

    const iconPath1 = $('.mbz-ad-icon-path-1');
    const iconPath2 = $('.mbz-ad-icon-path-2');

    function getThemeMeta(theme) {
      const t = String(theme || '').toLowerCase();

      if (t === 'disclosure') {
        return {
          bg1:'#ff7a18',
          bg2:'#ff5a3d',
          sub:'rgba(255,255,255,.84)',
          icon:'disclosure'
        };
      }

      if (t === 'newsroom') {
        return {
          bg1:'#2563eb',
          bg2:'#0ea5e9',
          sub:'rgba(255,255,255,.86)',
          icon:'newsroom'
        };
      }

      if (t === 'risk') {
        return {
          bg1:'#ef4444',
          bg2:'#f97316',
          sub:'rgba(255,255,255,.84)',
          icon:'risk'
        };
      }

      if (t === 'naver') {
        return {
          bg1:'#03c75a',
          bg2:'#11b86b',
          sub:'rgba(255,255,255,.86)',
          icon:'blog'
        };
      }

      if (t === 'report') {
        return {
          bg1:'#1e3a8a',
          bg2:'#4338ca',
          sub:'rgba(255,255,255,.86)',
          icon:'report'
        };
      }

      if (t === 'grape') {
        return {
          bg1:'#7c3aed',
          bg2:'#8b5cf6',
          sub:'rgba(255,255,255,.84)',
          icon:'premium'
        };
      }

      if (t === 'business') {
        return {
          bg1:'#0369a1',
          bg2:'#0d9488',
          sub:'rgba(255,255,255,.86)',
          icon:'business'
        };
      }

      return {
        bg1:'#14b8a6',
        bg2:'#0f766e',
        sub:'rgba(255,255,255,.84)',
        icon:'default'
      };
    }

    function applyTheme(ad) {
      const meta = getThemeMeta(ad && ad.theme);

      if (!link) return;

      link.style.setProperty('--mbz-bg1', meta.bg1);
      link.style.setProperty('--mbz-bg2', meta.bg2);
      link.style.setProperty('--mbz-sub', meta.sub);

      applyIcon(meta.icon);
    }

    function applyIcon(type) {
      if (!iconPath1 || !iconPath2) return;

      if (type === 'newsroom') {
        iconPath1.setAttribute('d', 'M7 8h10M7 12h8M7 16h10');
        iconPath2.setAttribute('d', 'M5 5h14v14H5z');
        return;
      }

      if (type === 'risk') {
        iconPath1.setAttribute('d', 'M12 8v5M12 17h.01');
        iconPath2.setAttribute('d', 'M12 3l9 16H3z');
        return;
      }

      if (type === 'blog') {
        iconPath1.setAttribute('d', 'M8 9h8M8 13h6');
        iconPath2.setAttribute('d', 'M5 5h14v11H9l-4 4z');
        return;
      }

      if (type === 'report') {
        iconPath1.setAttribute('d', 'M7 8h10M7 12h10M7 16h7');
        iconPath2.setAttribute('d', 'M5 4h14v16H5z');
        return;
      }

      if (type === 'premium') {
        iconPath1.setAttribute('d', 'M8 12l3 3 6-7');
        iconPath2.setAttribute('d', 'M12 3l8 4v5c0 5-3.5 8-8 9-4.5-1-8-4-8-9V7z');
        return;
      }

      if (type === 'business') {
        iconPath1.setAttribute('d', 'M8 8h8M8 12h5M9 17h6');
        iconPath2.setAttribute('d', 'M6 4h12v16H6z');
        return;
      }

      iconPath1.setAttribute('d', 'M7 7h10M7 12h10M7 17h6');
      iconPath2.setAttribute('d', 'M5 4h14v16H5z');
    }

    function setLinkHrefOrDisable(hrefRaw) {
      if (!link) return;

      const val = (typeof hrefRaw === 'string') ? hrefRaw.trim() : '';

      if (!val) {
        link.removeAttribute('href');
        link.removeAttribute('onclick');
        link.removeAttribute('target');
        link.removeAttribute('rel');
        link.classList.add('is-disabled');
        link.setAttribute('aria-disabled', 'true');
        return;
      }

      link.classList.remove('is-disabled');
      link.removeAttribute('aria-disabled');

      if (val.startsWith('onclick')) {
        const js = val
          .replace(/^onclick=['"]?/, '')
          .replace(/['"]?$/, '');

        link.removeAttribute('href');
        link.removeAttribute('target');
        link.removeAttribute('rel');
        link.setAttribute('onclick', js);
        return;
      }

      link.removeAttribute('onclick');
      link.setAttribute('href', val);

      if (/^https?:\/\//i.test(val)) {
        link.setAttribute('target', '_blank');
        link.setAttribute('rel', 'noopener noreferrer');
      } else {
        link.removeAttribute('target');
        link.removeAttribute('rel');
      }
    }

    function kickMotion() {
      if (wrap) {
        wrap.classList.remove('is-animating');
        void wrap.offsetWidth;
        wrap.classList.add('is-animating');
      }

      if (textBox) {
        textBox.classList.remove('is-changing');
        void textBox.offsetWidth;
        textBox.classList.add('is-changing');
      }
    }

    function render(ad) {
      setLinkHrefOrDisable(ad && ad.href);

      if (title) title.textContent = ad && ad.title ? ad.title : '';
      if (subtitle) subtitle.textContent = ad && ad.subtitle ? ad.subtitle : '';
      if (link) link.setAttribute('aria-label', ad && ad.title ? ('MarketBZ 프로모션: ' + ad.title) : 'MarketBZ 프로모션');

      applyTheme(ad || {});
      kickMotion();
    }

    function pickCandidates() {
      const now = nowKST();
      const valid = allAds.filter(function(ad) {
        return isActive(ad, now);
      });

      const base = (valid.length ? valid : allAds).slice();
      const fixed = base.filter(function(ad) { return !!ad.fixed; });
      const pool = (fixed.length ? fixed : base).slice();

      pool.sort(function(a, b) {
        return (b.priority || 0) - (a.priority || 0);
      });

      return pool;
    }

    function rebuildDots(total, active) {
      if (!dotsWrap) return;

      dotsWrap.innerHTML = '';

      const n = Math.max(total || 1, 1);

      for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        const b = document.createElement('button');
        b.type = 'button';
        b.className = 'mbz-ad-dot' + (i === active ? ' is-active' : '');
        b.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Go to ad ' + (i + 1));

        b.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
          e.preventDefault();
          e.stopPropagation();
          goTo(i, true);
        });

        dotsWrap.appendChild(b);
      }
    }

    function highlightDot(active) {
      if (!dotsWrap) return;

      Array.prototype.slice.call(dotsWrap.children).forEach(function(el, i) {
        if (i === active) el.classList.add('is-active');
        else el.classList.remove('is-active');
      });
    }

    function offsetIndex(pool) {
      if (!pool || !pool.length) return 0;
      return ((START_OFFSET % pool.length) + pool.length) % pool.length;
    }

    let idx = 0;
    let currentPool = pickCandidates();

    if (!currentPool.length) currentPool = allAds.slice();
    idx = offsetIndex(currentPool);

    if (currentPool.length) render(currentPool[idx]);
    rebuildDots(currentPool.length, idx);

    const INTERVAL_MS = 6000;
    const USER_PAUSE_MS = 10000;

    let lastUserActionAt = 0;
    let autoplay = null;

    const REDUCE_MOTION = typeof window !== 'undefined'
      && window.matchMedia
      && window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;

    function startAutoplay() {
      stopAutoplay();
      if (REDUCE_MOTION) return;

      autoplay = setInterval(function() {
        const pool = pickCandidates();

        const changed =
          JSON.stringify(pool.map(function(ad) { return ad.id; })) !==
          JSON.stringify(currentPool.map(function(ad) { return ad.id; }));

        if (changed) {
          currentPool = pool.length ? pool : allAds.slice();
          idx = offsetIndex(currentPool);
          render(currentPool[idx]);
          rebuildDots(currentPool.length, idx);
          return;
        }

        if (Date.now() - lastUserActionAt < USER_PAUSE_MS) return;

        if (currentPool.length > 1) {
          idx = (idx + 1) % currentPool.length;
          render(currentPool[idx]);
          highlightDot(idx);
        }
      }, INTERVAL_MS);
    }

    function stopAutoplay() {
      if (autoplay) clearInterval(autoplay);
      autoplay = null;
    }

    function goTo(i, user) {
      if (!currentPool.length) return;

      idx = ((i % currentPool.length) + currentPool.length) % currentPool.length;
      render(currentPool[idx]);
      highlightDot(idx);

      if (user) lastUserActionAt = Date.now();
    }

    startAutoplay();
  })();
  </script>
</section>

  <script>
  (function(){
    const s = document.currentScript;
    const root = s ? s.closest('section') : null;
    const START_OFFSET = root ? (parseInt(root.getAttribute('data-start-offset') || '0', 10) || 0) : 0;
    if (!root || root.dataset.init === '1') return;
    root.dataset.init = '1';

    const $ = (sel) => root.querySelector(sel);

    const RAW_TL = "[{\"id\":7,\"title\":\"Pro\u00B7Pro Plus \uAD6C\uB9E4 \uC774\uBCA4\uD2B8\",\"subtitle\":\"\uC720\uB8CC \uC774\uC0C1\uC9D5\uD6C4 \uBAA8\uB2C8\uD130\uB9C1 \uC54C\uB9BC \u00B7 \uD574\uB2F9 \uAD6C\uB9E4\uAE30\uAC04 \uBB34\uB8CC\",\"href\":\"\/goodsPlan#mbzProBenefitEvent\",\"startDate\":null,\"endDate\":\"2026-12-31\",\"startTime\":null,\"endTime\":null,\"fixed\":false,\"priority\":120,\"theme\":\"grape\"},{\"id\":8,\"title\":\"\uC804\uBB38 \uBD84\uC11D\uBCF4\uACE0\uC11C 6\uB300 \uC601\uC5ED\",\"subtitle\":\"\uAE30\uC5C5\u00B7HR\u00B7\uC2DC\uC7A5\u00B7\uC81C\uD488\u00B7\uC0B0\uC5C5\u00B7IPO \uBD84\uC11D\uC744 \uD55C \uBC88\uC5D0\",\"href\":\"\/goodsPlan#mbzReportCatalog\",\"startDate\":null,\"endDate\":null,\"startTime\":null,\"endTime\":null,\"fixed\":false,\"priority\":110,\"theme\":\"report\"},{\"id\":9,\"title\":\"\uAE30\uC5C5\uD68C\uC6D0 \uC778\uC99D \uD61C\uD0DD\",\"subtitle\":\"\uBA85\uD568 \uC778\uC99D \uD6C4 \uB300\uC0C1 \uC870\uD68C \uAD6C\uB3C5\uC0C1\uD488 \uAE30\uC5C5\uD68C\uC6D0\uAC00\",\"href\":\"\/goodsPlan#mbzBusinessMemberBenefit\",\"startDate\":null,\"endDate\":null,\"startTime\":null,\"endTime\":null,\"fixed\":false,\"priority\":108,\"theme\":\"business\"},{\"id\":1,\"title\":\"\uC804\uC790\uACF5\uC2DC \uD604\uD669 \uC624\uD508\",\"subtitle\":\"\uC8FC\uC694\uACF5\uC2DC\u00B7HOT\uACF5\uC2DC\uB97C \uD55C\uB208\uC5D0\",\"href\":\"onclick='return MBZCommonPopup.openDisclosurePayService();'\",\"startDate\":null,\"endDate\":null,\"startTime\":null,\"endTime\":null,\"fixed\":false,\"priority\":100,\"theme\":\"disclosure\"},{\"id\":2,\"title\":\"\uACF5\uC2DC\uD0D0\uC0C9 \uC624\uD508\",\"subtitle\":\"\uAE30\uC5C5 \uC774\uBCA4\uD2B8 \uBE60\uB978 \uD0D0\uC0C9\",\"href\":\"onclick='return MBZCommonPopup.openDisclosurePayService();'\",\"startDate\":null,\"endDate\":null,\"startTime\":null,\"endTime\":null,\"fixed\":false,\"priority\":96,\"theme\":\"disclosure\"},{\"id\":4,\"title\":\"\uC774\uC0C1\uC9D5\uD6C4 \uBAA8\uB2C8\uD130\uB9C1\",\"subtitle\":\"\uACF5\uC2DC\u00B7\uAE30\uC5C5\uC815\uBCF4 \uBCC0\uD654 \uC2E0\uD638\uC640 \uADFC\uAC70 \uD655\uC778\",\"href\":\"\/riskSignalStatus\",\"startDate\":null,\"endDate\":null,\"startTime\":null,\"endTime\":null,\"fixed\":false,\"priority\":92,\"theme\":\"risk\"},{\"id\":5,\"title\":\"\uB9C8\uCF13\uBE44\uC988 \uBE14\uB85C\uADF8\",\"subtitle\":\"\uAE30\uC5C5\uC815\uBCF4 \uB9DB\uC9D1 \uB9C8\uCF13\uBE44\uC988\",\"href\":\"https:\/\/blog.naver.com\/marketbz\",\"startDate\":null,\"endDate\":null,\"startTime\":null,\"endTime\":null,\"fixed\":false,\"priority\":80,\"theme\":\"naver\"},{\"id\":6,\"title\":\"\uAE30\uC5C5\uC815\uBCF4 Live\",\"subtitle\":\"\uC6D0\uCC9C\uBCC4 \uCD5C\uC2E0 \uAC31\uC2E0 \uAE30\uC5C5\uC815\uBCF4 \uD655\uC778\",\"href\":\"\/\uC5C5\uCCB4\uBA85\uC73C\uB85C\uAE30\uC5C5\uC870\uD68C\",\"startDate\":null,\"endDate\":null,\"startTime\":null,\"endTime\":null,\"fixed\":false,\"priority\":60,\"theme\":\"ocean\"}]";
    const RAW =
      (typeof INJECT !== 'undefined' && INJECT) ||
      (typeof window !== 'undefined' && typeof window.INJECT !== 'undefined' && window.INJECT) ||
      (typeof ADS_JSON !== 'undefined' && ADS_JSON) ||
      RAW_TL;

    const VERIFIED_BUSINESS_MEMBER = false;

    let allAds = [];
    try {
      allAds = Array.isArray(RAW) ? RAW : JSON.parse(RAW || '[]');
      if (!Array.isArray(allAds)) allAds = [];
      if (VERIFIED_BUSINESS_MEMBER) {
        allAds = allAds.filter(function(ad) {
          return String(ad && ad.theme || '').toLowerCase() !== 'business';
        });
      }
    } catch(e) {
      allAds = [];
    }

    function nowKST() {
      const n = new Date();
      const utc = n.getTime() + n.getTimezoneOffset() * 60000;
      return new Date(utc + 9 * 3600 * 1000);
    }

    function parseHM(hm) {
      if (!hm) return null;
      const m = String(hm).match(/^(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})(?::(\d{2}))?$/);
      if (!m) return null;
      return (+m[1]) * 60 + (+m[2]);
    }

    function parseYMD(s) {
      if (!s) return null;
      const m = String(s).match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})/);
      if (!m) return null;
      return { y:+m[1], m:+m[2]-1, d:+m[3] };
    }

    function inDateRange(ad, now) {
      const s = parseYMD(ad.startDate);
      const e = parseYMD(ad.endDate);
      if (!s && !e) return true;

      const d = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), now.getDate());

      if (s) {
        const sd = new Date(s.y, s.m, s.d);
        if (d < sd) return false;
      }

      if (e) {
        const ed = new Date(e.y, e.m, e.d);
        if (d > ed) return false;
      }

      return true;
    }

    function inTimeRange(ad, now) {
      const minutes = now.getHours() * 60 + now.getMinutes();
      const s = parseHM(ad.startTime);
      const e = parseHM(ad.endTime);

      if (s == null && e == null) return true;

      if (s != null && e != null) {
        return e >= s ? (minutes >= s && minutes <= e) : (minutes >= s || minutes <= e);
      }

      if (s != null) return minutes >= s;
      if (e != null) return minutes <= e;

      return true;
    }

    function isActive(ad, now) {
      return inDateRange(ad, now) && inTimeRange(ad, now);
    }

    const wrap = $('.mbz-ad-wrap');
    const link = $('.mbz-ad-link');
    const textBox = $('.mbz-ad-text');
    const title = $('.mbz-ad-title');
    const subtitle = $('.mbz-ad-subtitle');
    const dotsWrap = $('.mbz-ad-dots');

    const iconPath1 = $('.mbz-ad-icon-path-1');
    const iconPath2 = $('.mbz-ad-icon-path-2');

    function getThemeMeta(theme) {
      const t = String(theme || '').toLowerCase();

      if (t === 'disclosure') {
        return {
          bg1:'#ff7a18',
          bg2:'#ff5a3d',
          sub:'rgba(255,255,255,.84)',
          icon:'disclosure'
        };
      }

      if (t === 'newsroom') {
        return {
          bg1:'#2563eb',
          bg2:'#0ea5e9',
          sub:'rgba(255,255,255,.86)',
          icon:'newsroom'
        };
      }

      if (t === 'risk') {
        return {
          bg1:'#ef4444',
          bg2:'#f97316',
          sub:'rgba(255,255,255,.84)',
          icon:'risk'
        };
      }

      if (t === 'naver') {
        return {
          bg1:'#03c75a',
          bg2:'#11b86b',
          sub:'rgba(255,255,255,.86)',
          icon:'blog'
        };
      }

      if (t === 'report') {
        return {
          bg1:'#1e3a8a',
          bg2:'#4338ca',
          sub:'rgba(255,255,255,.86)',
          icon:'report'
        };
      }

      if (t === 'grape') {
        return {
          bg1:'#7c3aed',
          bg2:'#8b5cf6',
          sub:'rgba(255,255,255,.84)',
          icon:'premium'
        };
      }

      if (t === 'business') {
        return {
          bg1:'#0369a1',
          bg2:'#0d9488',
          sub:'rgba(255,255,255,.86)',
          icon:'business'
        };
      }

      return {
        bg1:'#14b8a6',
        bg2:'#0f766e',
        sub:'rgba(255,255,255,.84)',
        icon:'default'
      };
    }

    function applyTheme(ad) {
      const meta = getThemeMeta(ad && ad.theme);

      if (!link) return;

      link.style.setProperty('--mbz-bg1', meta.bg1);
      link.style.setProperty('--mbz-bg2', meta.bg2);
      link.style.setProperty('--mbz-sub', meta.sub);

      applyIcon(meta.icon);
    }

    function applyIcon(type) {
      if (!iconPath1 || !iconPath2) return;

      if (type === 'newsroom') {
        iconPath1.setAttribute('d', 'M7 8h10M7 12h8M7 16h10');
        iconPath2.setAttribute('d', 'M5 5h14v14H5z');
        return;
      }

      if (type === 'risk') {
        iconPath1.setAttribute('d', 'M12 8v5M12 17h.01');
        iconPath2.setAttribute('d', 'M12 3l9 16H3z');
        return;
      }

      if (type === 'blog') {
        iconPath1.setAttribute('d', 'M8 9h8M8 13h6');
        iconPath2.setAttribute('d', 'M5 5h14v11H9l-4 4z');
        return;
      }

      if (type === 'report') {
        iconPath1.setAttribute('d', 'M7 8h10M7 12h10M7 16h7');
        iconPath2.setAttribute('d', 'M5 4h14v16H5z');
        return;
      }

      if (type === 'premium') {
        iconPath1.setAttribute('d', 'M8 12l3 3 6-7');
        iconPath2.setAttribute('d', 'M12 3l8 4v5c0 5-3.5 8-8 9-4.5-1-8-4-8-9V7z');
        return;
      }

      if (type === 'business') {
        iconPath1.setAttribute('d', 'M8 8h8M8 12h5M9 17h6');
        iconPath2.setAttribute('d', 'M6 4h12v16H6z');
        return;
      }

      iconPath1.setAttribute('d', 'M7 7h10M7 12h10M7 17h6');
      iconPath2.setAttribute('d', 'M5 4h14v16H5z');
    }

    function setLinkHrefOrDisable(hrefRaw) {
      if (!link) return;

      const val = (typeof hrefRaw === 'string') ? hrefRaw.trim() : '';

      if (!val) {
        link.removeAttribute('href');
        link.removeAttribute('onclick');
        link.removeAttribute('target');
        link.removeAttribute('rel');
        link.classList.add('is-disabled');
        link.setAttribute('aria-disabled', 'true');
        return;
      }

      link.classList.remove('is-disabled');
      link.removeAttribute('aria-disabled');

      if (val.startsWith('onclick')) {
        const js = val
          .replace(/^onclick=['"]?/, '')
          .replace(/['"]?$/, '');

        link.removeAttribute('href');
        link.removeAttribute('target');
        link.removeAttribute('rel');
        link.setAttribute('onclick', js);
        return;
      }

      link.removeAttribute('onclick');
      link.setAttribute('href', val);

      if (/^https?:\/\//i.test(val)) {
        link.setAttribute('target', '_blank');
        link.setAttribute('rel', 'noopener noreferrer');
      } else {
        link.removeAttribute('target');
        link.removeAttribute('rel');
      }
    }

    function kickMotion() {
      if (wrap) {
        wrap.classList.remove('is-animating');
        void wrap.offsetWidth;
        wrap.classList.add('is-animating');
      }

      if (textBox) {
        textBox.classList.remove('is-changing');
        void textBox.offsetWidth;
        textBox.classList.add('is-changing');
      }
    }

    function render(ad) {
      setLinkHrefOrDisable(ad && ad.href);

      if (title) title.textContent = ad && ad.title ? ad.title : '';
      if (subtitle) subtitle.textContent = ad && ad.subtitle ? ad.subtitle : '';
      if (link) link.setAttribute('aria-label', ad && ad.title ? ('MarketBZ 프로모션: ' + ad.title) : 'MarketBZ 프로모션');

      applyTheme(ad || {});
      kickMotion();
    }

    function pickCandidates() {
      const now = nowKST();
      const valid = allAds.filter(function(ad) {
        return isActive(ad, now);
      });

      const base = (valid.length ? valid : allAds).slice();
      const fixed = base.filter(function(ad) { return !!ad.fixed; });
      const pool = (fixed.length ? fixed : base).slice();

      pool.sort(function(a, b) {
        return (b.priority || 0) - (a.priority || 0);
      });

      return pool;
    }

    function rebuildDots(total, active) {
      if (!dotsWrap) return;

      dotsWrap.innerHTML = '';

      const n = Math.max(total || 1, 1);

      for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        const b = document.createElement('button');
        b.type = 'button';
        b.className = 'mbz-ad-dot' + (i === active ? ' is-active' : '');
        b.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Go to ad ' + (i + 1));

        b.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
          e.preventDefault();
          e.stopPropagation();
          goTo(i, true);
        });

        dotsWrap.appendChild(b);
      }
    }

    function highlightDot(active) {
      if (!dotsWrap) return;

      Array.prototype.slice.call(dotsWrap.children).forEach(function(el, i) {
        if (i === active) el.classList.add('is-active');
        else el.classList.remove('is-active');
      });
    }

    function offsetIndex(pool) {
      if (!pool || !pool.length) return 0;
      return ((START_OFFSET % pool.length) + pool.length) % pool.length;
    }

    let idx = 0;
    let currentPool = pickCandidates();

    if (!currentPool.length) currentPool = allAds.slice();
    idx = offsetIndex(currentPool);

    if (currentPool.length) render(currentPool[idx]);
    rebuildDots(currentPool.length, idx);

    const INTERVAL_MS = 6000;
    const USER_PAUSE_MS = 10000;

    let lastUserActionAt = 0;
    let autoplay = null;

    const REDUCE_MOTION = typeof window !== 'undefined'
      && window.matchMedia
      && window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;

    function startAutoplay() {
      stopAutoplay();
      if (REDUCE_MOTION) return;

      autoplay = setInterval(function() {
        const pool = pickCandidates();

        const changed =
          JSON.stringify(pool.map(function(ad) { return ad.id; })) !==
          JSON.stringify(currentPool.map(function(ad) { return ad.id; }));

        if (changed) {
          currentPool = pool.length ? pool : allAds.slice();
          idx = offsetIndex(currentPool);
          render(currentPool[idx]);
          rebuildDots(currentPool.length, idx);
          return;
        }

        if (Date.now() - lastUserActionAt < USER_PAUSE_MS) return;

        if (currentPool.length > 1) {
          idx = (idx + 1) % currentPool.length;
          render(currentPool[idx]);
          highlightDot(idx);
        }
      }, INTERVAL_MS);
    }

    function stopAutoplay() {
      if (autoplay) clearInterval(autoplay);
      autoplay = null;
    }

    function goTo(i, user) {
      if (!currentPool.length) return;

      idx = ((i % currentPool.length) + currentPool.length) % currentPool.length;
      render(currentPool[idx]);
      highlightDot(idx);

      if (user) lastUserActionAt = Date.now();
    }

    startAutoplay();
  })();
  </script>
</section>

                <script>
                    $(function () {
                        var isPdfRender = /[?&]mbzPdfRender=1(?:&|$)/.test(window.location.search || '');
                        if (!isPdfRender && typeof mbzInitRecentCompanies === 'function') {
                            mbzInitRecentCompanies();

                <script type="text/javascript" src="/type/common/js/company-detail-pdf-runtime-r79.js?ver=20261010195546&amp;mbzperf=20260902-r79"></script>

				

				<script>
				function goBackOpener() {
					console.log(window.opener);
				    if (window.opener && !window.opener.closed) {
				        window.opener.focus(); // 부모 창에서 뒤로 가기 실행
				        window.close(); // 현재 창 닫기 (필요할 경우)
				    } else {
				    	history.back(); // 부모 창이 없으면 현재 창에서 뒤로 가기
				    }
				}

				$(document).ready(function() {
					$('.btn-share').click(function() {

				    	var title = $(this).data('title');

						// Web Share API 지원 여부 확인
						if (navigator.share) {
							navigator.share({
								title: title,
								text: '이 웹 페이지를 공유해보세요!',
								url: window.location.href,
							})
							.then(() => console.log('공유 성공'))
							.catch((err) => console.error('공유 실패:', err));
						} else {
							// 클립보드에 텍스트 복사를 위한 임시 텍스트 에리어 생성 및 설정
							var $temp = $("<input>");
							$("body").append($temp);
							$temp.val(window.location.href).select();
							try {
								document.execCommand("copy");
								$temp.remove();
								alert('URL이 복사되었습니다.');
							} catch (err) {
								console.error('클립보드 복사 실패:', err);
								alert('복사에 실패했습니다.');
								$temp.remove();
							}
						}
					});

                    $('.btn-forbidden').click(async function() {
                        const session = await mbzCheckSession1();
                        if(!session || !session.ok) {
                            return false;
                        } else {
                            ifrPopClose('LoginPop');
                            ifrPopClose('FindEmailPop');
                            ifrPopClose('SubScriptApliPop');
                            ifrPopClose('OnePop');
                            $("body").css("overflow-y", "hidden");

                            var b_no = this.getAttribute("data-id");
                            var ifrSrc = "/mbz/bizauth/" + b_no;
                            $('<iframe id="iframeOnePop" name="iframeOnePop" class="pop_iframe on" src="' + ifrSrc + '" frameborder="0" style="left: 0;border: 0px currentColor; border-image: none; top: 0px; width: 100%; height: 100vh; position: fixed; z-index: 999999; backdrop-filter: blur(3px);"/>').appendTo($("body"));
                        }
                    });
				});
				</script>
				
				<div class="mbz_refresh_btn">
					<div class="circle blue"></div>
					<div class="circle purple"></div>
					<div class="circle pink"></div>
					<div class="circle red"></div>
					<div class="circle yellow"></div>
				</div>

                        /* 2026-09-29 UI-R1G - natural mobile identity line flow.
                           R1F compacted the finance/status tail, but flex still treats a wrapped H1 as one

                           and pushes finance/status to line 3. Mobile only: switch the confirmed-name row to

                        body.mbz-company-detail-page #contents .main_company_detail

    <!-- UI-R2A-H5: MarketBZ-wide feed is a GLOBAL rail, not company-specific content.
         Keep the original fragment/auth/event contract; only its placement changes. -->
    
    <!-- 2026-09-29 UI-R2A-H5
         MarketBZ-wide disclosure/blog feed. This is deliberately a compact GLOBAL rail above company
         identity, not a fact about the currently viewed company. Existing blog-rss authorization/events
         remain authoritative. -->

      동적 문자열을 JavaScript 소스에 직접 삽입하지 않는다.

      업체명 따옴표/개행/</script> 문자열, JSON null·불완전값으로 인한 문법 오류를 차단한다.
    -->
    <span hidden
          data-mbz-company-client-meta="true" data-company-name="주식회사 트윈랩" data-business-number="6168628898" data-formatted-business-number="616-86-28898"></span>
    <script>
        (function () {
            var script = document.currentScript;
            var el = script ? script.previousElementSibling : null;
            if (!el || !el.hasAttribute('data-mbz-company-client-meta')) {
                return;
            }
            window.mbzCompanyClientMeta = Object.freeze({
                companyName: String(el.dataset.companyName || ''),
                businessNumber: String(el.dataset.businessNumber || ''),
                formattedBusinessNumber: String(el.dataset.formattedBusinessNumber || ''),
                changeHistoryJson: String(el.dataset.changeHistoryJson || '')
            });
        })();
    </script>

            <input type="hidden" id="listBadge" name="listBadge" value="" />

            <div class="mbz-action-row">
                <button type="button" id="mbzHelpReopenBtn" class="mbz-help-reopen hidden">도움말</button>
                <button type="button" id="mbzBadgeGoBtn" class="mbz-go-btn hidden">유사기업 조회</button>
            </div>

            <div id="mbzBadgeToast" class="mbz-toast">
                배지를 3개 이상 선택하면 유사기업 조회가 가능합니다.
            </div>

            <link rel="stylesheet" type="text/css" href="/type/common/css/mbz-company-badge-list-perf-r46.css?ver=20261010195546&amp;mbzperf=20261001-h6iz-mobile-rhythm" />

            <script>
                (function(){
                    var $root = $(".mbz-badge-root").last();
                    if(!$root.length) return;

                    $root.off(".mbzBadge");

                    var $tray  = $root.find("#mbzSelectedTray");
                    var $chips = $root.find("#mbzSelectedChips");
                    var $clear = $root.find("#mbzClearAllBtn");
                    var $cta   = $root.find("#mbzBadgeGoBtn");
                    var $toast = $root.find("#mbzBadgeToast");
                    var $helpBubble = $root.find("#mbzHelpBubble");
                    var $helpClose = $root.find("#mbzHelpCloseBtn");
                    var $helpReopen = $root.find("#mbzHelpReopenBtn");

                    window.gBadgeSelectedArr = window.gBadgeSelectedArr || [];

                    function getSelectedButtons(){
                        return $root.find(".badgeBtn.mbz-active").not(".badgeBtn-lock");
                    }

                    function getSearchBadgeIds(){
                        var set = new Set();
                        gBadgeSelectedArr = [];

                        getSelectedButtons().each(function(){
                            var id = $(this).data("id");
                            if(!id) return;
                            set.add(String(id));
                            gBadgeSelectedArr.push(this.outerHTML.replace('badgeBtn',''));
                        });

                        return Array.from(set);
                    }

                    var toastTimer = null;
                    function showToast(msg){
                        $toast.text(msg).addClass("show");
                        clearTimeout(toastTimer);
                        toastTimer = setTimeout(function(){
                            $toast.removeClass("show");
                        }, 1600);
                    }

                    function renderSelectedTray(){
                        var $active = getSelectedButtons();
                        $chips.empty();

                        if($active.length === 0){
                            $tray.removeClass("show-flex").addClass("hidden");
                            return;
                        }

                        $tray.removeClass("hidden").addClass("show-flex");

                        $active.each(function(){
                            var $btn = $(this);
                            var id   = $btn.data("id");
                            var name = $btn.data("name") || $btn.find(".badge-name").text();

                            var $chip = $('<span class="mbz-chip" data-id="'+id+'"></span>');
                            $chip.append(document.createTextNode(name));
                            $chip.append('<span class="x">×</span>');
                            $chips.append($chip);
                        });

                        var onlyDefault = ($active.length === 1 && $active.first().data("group") === "BG0025");
                        $clear.prop("disabled", onlyDefault);
                    }

                    function updateCTA(){
                        var cnt = getSelectedButtons().length;
                        if(cnt >= 3){
                            $cta.removeClass("hidden").prop("disabled", false);
                        }else if(cnt === 1 || cnt === 2){
                            $cta.removeClass("hidden").prop("disabled", true);
                        }else{
                            $cta.addClass("hidden").prop("disabled", true);
                        }
                    }

                    function syncUI(lastAction){
                        renderSelectedTray();
                        updateCTA();

                        var cnt = getSelectedButtons().length;
                        if(lastAction === "toggle" && cnt < 3){
                            showToast("배지를 3개 이상 선택하면 유사기업 조회가 가능합니다.");
                        }
                    }

                    function clearAllBadges(keepDefault){
                        getSelectedButtons().each(function(){
                            var $btn = $(this);
                            var isDefault = ($btn.data("group") === "BG0025");
                            if(keepDefault && isDefault) return;
                            $btn.removeClass("mbz-active");
                        });
                        syncUI("clearall");
                    }

                    $root.on("click.mbzBadge", ".badgeBtn", function(){
                        var $btn = $(this);

                        if($btn.hasClass("badgeBtn-lock")) return;

                        $btn.removeClass("blinking");
                        $btn.toggleClass("mbz-active");
                        syncUI("toggle");
                    });

                    $root.on("click.mbzBadge", ".mbz-chip", function(){
                        var id = $(this).data("id");
                        $root.find('.badgeBtn[data-id="'+id+'"]').removeClass("mbz-active");
                        syncUI("chip");
                    });

                    $root.on("click.mbzBadge", "#mbzClearAllBtn", function(){
                        clearAllBadges(true);
                    });

                    $root.on("click.mbzBadge", "#mbzBadgeGoBtn", function(){
                        var ids = getSearchBadgeIds();
                        $root.find("#listBadge").val(ids.join(","));

                        $(".loading-wrap-02").show();
                        if(ids.length >= 3){
                            top.location.href = "/배지로기업조회/" + ids.join(",");
                        }else{
                            fn_alertModalOpen("배지를 3개 이상 선택해 주세요");
                            $(".loading-wrap-02").hide();
                        }
                    });

                    $helpClose.on("click", function(){
                        $helpBubble.addClass("hidden");
                        $helpReopen.removeClass("hidden");
                    });

                    $helpReopen.on("click", function(){
                        $helpBubble.removeClass("hidden");
                        $helpReopen.addClass("hidden");
                    });

                    $(function(){
                        syncUI("init");
                        setTimeout(function(){
                            $root.find('.badgeBtn.blinking[data-group="BG0025"]').removeClass("blinking");
                        }, 4500);
                    });
                })();
            </script>
        </div>
    </div>

                                <div class="mbz-buy-zone mbz-buy-zone-free"
                                     data-nosnippet
                                     aria-label="기업상세 구매 및 업그레이드">

                                <!-- 구독 업그레이드 -->
                                <div class="mbz-buy-section is-subscription">
                                    <div class="mbz-buy-section-head">
                                        <strong>구독 상품</strong>
                                        <span>현재 권한에서 구매 가능한 상품만 표시합니다.</span>
                                    </div>

    <style>
        [data-rbz], [data-rbz] * { box-sizing:border-box; }
        [data-rbz] { width:100%; min-width:0; }
        [data-rbz] .rbz-shell {
            width:100%;
            overflow:hidden;
            border:1px solid #d8e2ee;
            border-radius:13px;
            background:#fff;
            box-shadow:0 5px 14px rgba(15,23,42,.065);

            align-items:center;
            justify-content:space-between;
            gap:5px;
            min-height:31px;
            padding:7px 8px 5px;
            border-bottom:1px solid #e8edf4;
            background:linear-gradient(135deg,#fbfdff 0%,#f2f7ff 100%);
        }
        [data-rbz] .rbz-head-copy {
            display:grid;
            grid-template-columns:minmax(0,1fr);
            gap:2px;
            min-width:0;
        }

            color:#ea580c;
            font-size:6.8px;
            font-weight:950;
            line-height:1;
            letter-spacing:.08em;
            white-space:nowrap;
        }
        [data-rbz] .rbz-title {
            overflow:hidden;
            color:#0f172a;
            font-size:11.2px;
            font-weight:950;
            letter-spacing:-.03em;
            text-overflow:ellipsis;
            white-space:nowrap;
        }
        [data-rbz] .rbz-access {
            flex:0 0 auto;
            padding:1px 5px;
            border:1px solid #cbdaf8;
            border-radius:999px;
            background:#eef4ff;
            color:#2563eb;
            font-size:7.2px;
            font-weight:900;
            line-height:1.45;
        }
        [data-rbz] .rbz-tabs {
            display:grid;
            grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;
            gap:3px;
            padding:4px 5px;
            border-bottom:1px solid #edf1f6;
            background:#fbfdff;
        }
        [data-rbz] .rbz-tab {
            min-width:0;
            height:23px;
            border:1px solid transparent;
            border-radius:7px;
            background:transparent;
            color:#64748b;
            font-size:8.2px;
            font-weight:900;
            cursor:pointer;
            transition:background-color .18s ease,color .18s ease,border-color .18s ease;
        }
        [data-rbz] .rbz-tab:hover { background:#f1f5f9; color:#334155; }
        [data-rbz] .rbz-tab.is-active {
            border-color:#d5e2fb;
            background:#edf4ff;
            color:#1d4ed8;
        }
        [data-rbz] .rbz-pane[hidden] { display:none !important; }
        [data-rbz] .rbz-list { display:grid; }
        [data-rbz] .rbz-row {
            --rbz-rank-color:#475569;
            position:relative;
            min-height:60px;
            padding:6px 6px 6px 8px;
            border-bottom:1px solid #edf1f6;
            background:#fff;
            transition:background-color .18s ease;
        }
        [data-rbz] .rbz-row:hover { background:#fbfdff; }
        [data-rbz] .rbz-row:last-child { border-bottom:0; }

        [data-rbz] .rbz-row.is-elite {
            background:linear-gradient(90deg,rgba(190,24,93,.045) 0%,rgba(255,255,255,0) 74%), #fff;
        }
        [data-rbz] .rbz-row.is-elite:hover {
            background:linear-gradient(90deg,rgba(190,24,93,.06) 0%,rgba(255,255,255,0) 74%), #fbfdff;
        }
        [data-rbz] .rbz-value.is-elite {
            color:#be123c;
            letter-spacing:-.05em;
        }
        [data-rbz] .rbz-row.is-elite .rbz-arc-center {
            fill:#be123c;
            font-weight:950;
        }
        [data-rbz] .rbz-row::before {
            position:absolute;
            top:7px;
            bottom:7px;
            left:0;
            width:2px;
            border-radius:0 3px 3px 0;
            background:var(--rbz-accent,#2563eb);
            content:"";
        }
        [data-rbz] .rbz-label-wrap {
            min-width:0;
            display:flex;
            align-items:center;
            justify-content:space-between;
            gap:3px;
            margin-bottom:2px;

            color:#27364a;

            letter-spacing:-.035em;

        [data-rbz] .rbz-source {

            flex:0 0 auto;
            padding:1px 3px;
            border:1px solid #e1e7ef;
            border-radius:999px;

            color:#64748b;
            font-size:5.7px;
            font-weight:850;
            line-height:1.35;
        }
        [data-rbz] .rbz-body {
            display:grid;
            grid-template-columns:48px minmax(0,1fr);
            align-items:center;
            gap:4px;
            min-width:0;
        }
        [data-rbz] .rbz-mini {
            display:block;
            width:48px;
            height:30px;
            overflow:visible;
        }
        [data-rbz] .rbz-arc-track {
            fill:none;
            stroke:#e5eaf1;
            stroke-width:4.2;
            stroke-linecap:round;
        }
        [data-rbz] .rbz-arc-tick {
            stroke:#cbd5e1;
            stroke-width:1;
            stroke-linecap:round;
        }
        [data-rbz] .rbz-arc-progress {
            fill:none;
            stroke:var(--rbz-rank-color);
            stroke-width:4.2;
            stroke-linecap:round;
            stroke-dasharray:100;
            stroke-dashoffset:100;
            transition:stroke-dashoffset .72s cubic-bezier(.22,.8,.28,1),stroke .18s ease;
        }
        [data-rbz] .rbz-arc-marker {
            fill:#fff;
            stroke:var(--rbz-rank-color);
            stroke-width:2.2;
            filter:drop-shadow(0 1px 1px rgba(15,23,42,.24));
        }
        [data-rbz] .rbz-arc-center {
            fill:#94a3b8;
            font-size:5.1px;
            font-weight:900;
            text-anchor:middle;
        }
        [data-rbz] .rbz-copy { min-width:0; }
        [data-rbz] .rbz-value {
            display:block;
            overflow:hidden;
            color:var(--rbz-rank-color);
            font-size:11.4px;
            font-weight:950;
            line-height:1.05;
            letter-spacing:-.055em;
            text-overflow:ellipsis;
            white-space:nowrap;
        }
        [data-rbz] .rbz-meta {
            display:flex;
            align-items:center;
            justify-content:space-between;
            gap:4px;
            min-width:0;
            margin-top:2px;
            color:#64748b;
            font-size:7px;
            font-weight:800;
            line-height:1.2;
        }
        [data-rbz] .rbz-exact {
            overflow:hidden;
            min-width:0;
            text-overflow:ellipsis;
            white-space:nowrap;
        }
        [data-rbz] .rbz-basis { flex:0 0 auto; color:#94a3b8; }
        [data-rbz] .rbz-empty {
            display:grid;
            grid-template-columns:42px minmax(0,1fr);
            align-items:center;
            gap:5px;
            min-height:31px;
        }
        [data-rbz] .rbz-placeholder {
            position:relative;
            width:40px;
            height:21px;
            border-top:4px solid #e5eaf1;
            border-radius:50% 50% 0 0;
        }
        [data-rbz] .rbz-placeholder::after {
            position:absolute;
            top:-7px;
            right:2px;
            width:7px;
            height:7px;
            border:2px solid #94a3b8;
            border-radius:50%;
            background:#fff;
            content:"";
        }
        [data-rbz] .rbz-no-data {
            color:#94a3b8;
            font-size:7px;
            font-weight:850;
            line-height:1.25;
        }
        [data-rbz] .rbz-state {
            --rbz-state-color:#64748b;
            --rbz-state-bg:#f8fafc;
            --rbz-state-border:#dbe4ef;
            position:relative;
            display:flex;
            align-items:center;
            justify-content:flex-start;
            gap:7px;
            width:100%;
            min-height:42px;
            padding:6px 7px;
            text-align:left;
            overflow:hidden;
            border:1px solid var(--rbz-state-border);
            border-radius:10px;
            background:var(--rbz-state-bg);
        }
        [data-rbz] .rbz-state::after {
            position:absolute;
            inset:0;
            pointer-events:none;
            background:linear-gradient(120deg,rgba(255,255,255,.24),transparent 50%);
            content:"";
        }
        [data-rbz] .rbz-state.is-free {
            --rbz-state-color:#2563eb;
            --rbz-state-bg:linear-gradient(135deg,#eff6ff,#f8fbff);
            --rbz-state-border:#bfdbfe;
        }
        [data-rbz] .rbz-state.is-standard {
            --rbz-state-color:#16a34a;
            --rbz-state-bg:linear-gradient(135deg,#f0fdf4,#f8fff9);
            --rbz-state-border:#bbf7d0;
        }
        [data-rbz] .rbz-state.is-premium {
            --rbz-state-color:#ea580c;
            --rbz-state-bg:linear-gradient(135deg,#fff7ed,#fffaf5);
            --rbz-state-border:#fdba74;
        }
        [data-rbz] .rbz-state.is-pro {
            --rbz-state-color:#6d28d9;
            --rbz-state-bg:linear-gradient(135deg,#f5f3ff,#fbfaff);
            --rbz-state-border:#c4b5fd;
        }
        [data-rbz] .rbz-state.is-unavailable {
            --rbz-state-color:#64748b;
            --rbz-state-bg:linear-gradient(135deg,#f8fafc,#f1f5f9);
            --rbz-state-border:#cbd5e1;
        }
        [data-rbz] .rbz-state-icon {
            position:relative;
            z-index:1;
            display:grid;
            width:28px;
            height:30px;
            place-items:center;
            border:1px solid color-mix(in srgb,var(--rbz-state-color) 24%,transparent);
            border-radius:9px;
            background:color-mix(in srgb,var(--rbz-state-color) 11%,#fff);
            color:var(--rbz-state-color);
        }
        [data-rbz] .rbz-state-icon svg { width:15px; height:15px; }
        [data-rbz] .rbz-state-copy { position:relative; z-index:1; flex:1 1 auto; min-width:0; text-align:left; }
        [data-rbz] .rbz-state-title {
            display:block;
            color:var(--rbz-state-color);
            font-size:9.6px;
            font-weight:950;
            line-height:1.05;
        }
        [data-rbz] .rbz-state-meta {
            display:flex;
            align-items:center;
            gap:3px;
            margin-top:3px;
            color:#64748b;
            font-size:6.9px;
            font-weight:850;
            line-height:1.2;
        }
        [data-rbz] .rbz-state-cta {
            position:relative;
            z-index:2;
            display:inline-flex;
            align-items:center;
            justify-content:center;
            width:100%;
            min-height:18px;
            padding:2px 6px;
            border:1px solid color-mix(in srgb,var(--rbz-state-color) 34%,transparent);
            border-radius:6px;
            background:#fff;
            color:var(--rbz-state-color);
            box-shadow:0 1px 3px color-mix(in srgb,var(--rbz-state-color) 10%,transparent);
            font-family:inherit;
            font-size:7.2px;
            font-weight:950;
            line-height:1;
            text-align:center;
            cursor:pointer;
            white-space:nowrap;
            transition:background-color .14s ease,border-color .14s ease,box-shadow .14s ease,transform .14s ease;
        }
        [data-rbz] .rbz-state-cta:hover,
        [data-rbz] .rbz-state-cta:focus-visible {
            border-color:color-mix(in srgb,var(--rbz-state-color) 58%,transparent);
            background:color-mix(in srgb,var(--rbz-state-color) 7%,#fff);
            box-shadow:0 3px 7px color-mix(in srgb,var(--rbz-state-color) 14%,transparent);
            transform:translateY(-1px);
            outline:none;
        }
        [data-rbz] .rbz-state-cta:focus-visible {
            outline:2px solid color-mix(in srgb,var(--rbz-state-color) 38%,transparent);
            outline-offset:1px;
        }
        [data-rbz] .rbz-unavailable { background:#fff; }

        @media (min-width:768px) and (max-width:1099.98px) {
            [data-rbz] .rbz-source { display:inline-flex; }
            [data-rbz] .rbz-label { font-size:10.3px; }
            [data-rbz] .rbz-body { grid-template-columns:60px minmax(0,1fr); gap:7px; }
            [data-rbz] .rbz-mini { width:60px; height:35px; }
            [data-rbz] .rbz-value { font-size:12.4px; }
            [data-rbz] .rbz-meta { font-size:8px; }
            [data-rbz] .rbz-state-cta { min-height:20px; font-size:8px; }
        }
        @media (max-width:767.98px) {
            [data-rbz] .rbz-tabs { grid-template-columns:repeat(2,minmax(0,1fr)); }
            [data-rbz] .rbz-list { grid-template-columns:repeat(3,minmax(0,1fr)); }
            [data-rbz] .rbz-row { min-height:76px; border-right:1px solid #edf1f6; }
            [data-rbz] .rbz-source { display:inline-flex; }
            [data-rbz] .rbz-label { font-size:10.3px; }
            [data-rbz] .rbz-body { grid-template-columns:54px minmax(0,1fr); gap:6px; }
            [data-rbz] .rbz-mini { width:54px; height:32px; }
            [data-rbz] .rbz-value { font-size:12.4px; }
            [data-rbz] .rbz-meta { font-size:7.5px; }
            [data-rbz] .rbz-state-cta { min-height:22px; font-size:8.4px; }
        }
        @media (max-width:520px) {
            [data-rbz] .rbz-list { grid-template-columns:1fr; }
            [data-rbz] .rbz-row { min-height:61px; border-right:0; }
            [data-rbz] .rbz-body { grid-template-columns:60px minmax(0,1fr); }
            [data-rbz] .rbz-mini { width:60px; height:35px; }
        }
        @media (prefers-reduced-motion:reduce) {
            [data-rbz] .rbz-arc-progress,
            [data-rbz] .rbz-tab,
            [data-rbz] .rbz-row { transition:none !important; }
        }

        body.darkmode [data-rbz] .rbz-shell,
        body.darkmode [data-rbz] .rbz-row { border-color:#334155; background:#111827; }
        body.darkmode [data-rbz] .rbz-row:hover { background:#131d2d; }
        body.darkmode [data-rbz] .rbz-row.is-elite { background:linear-gradient(90deg,rgba(190,24,93,.10) 0%,rgba(17,24,39,0) 72%), #111827; }
        body.darkmode [data-rbz] .rbz-row.is-elite:hover { background:linear-gradient(90deg,rgba(190,24,93,.14) 0%,rgba(19,29,45,0) 72%), #131d2d; }
        body.darkmode [data-rbz] .rbz-head,
        body.darkmode [data-rbz] .rbz-tabs { border-color:#334155; background:linear-gradient(135deg,#172033,#111827); }
        body.darkmode [data-rbz] .rbz-tab { color:#94a3b8; }
        body.darkmode [data-rbz] .rbz-tab:hover { background:#1e293b; color:#e2e8f0; }
        body.darkmode [data-rbz] .rbz-tab.is-active { border-color:#475569; background:#1e3a5f; color:#bfdbfe; }
        body.darkmode [data-rbz] .rbz-title { color:#f8fafc; }
        body.darkmode [data-rbz] .rbz-kicker { color:#fb923c; }
        body.darkmode [data-rbz] .rbz-label { color:#dbeafe; }
        body.darkmode [data-rbz] .rbz-meta { color:#94a3b8; }
        body.darkmode [data-rbz] .rbz-source { border-color:#475569; background:#1e293b; color:#cbd5e1; }
        body.darkmode [data-rbz] .rbz-arc-track { stroke:#334155; }
        body.darkmode [data-rbz] .rbz-arc-tick { stroke:#475569; }
        body.darkmode [data-rbz] .rbz-arc-marker { fill:#111827; }
        body.darkmode [data-rbz] .rbz-placeholder { border-color:#334155; }
        body.darkmode [data-rbz] .rbz-placeholder::after { background:#111827; }
        body.darkmode [data-rbz] .rbz-unavailable { background:#111827; }
        body.darkmode [data-rbz] .rbz-state {
            --rbz-state-bg:#172033;
            --rbz-state-border:#334155;
        }
        body.darkmode [data-rbz] .rbz-state-icon { background:#111827; }
        body.darkmode [data-rbz] .rbz-state-meta { color:#94a3b8; }
        body.darkmode [data-rbz] .rbz-state.is-standard .rbz-state-cta {
            border-color:rgba(74,222,128,.42);
            background:rgba(22,163,74,.12);
            color:#86efac;
            box-shadow:none;

        body.darkmode [data-rbz] .rbz-state.is-premium .rbz-state-cta {
            border-color:rgba(251,146,60,.45);
            background:rgba(249,115,22,.12);
            color:#fdba74;
            box-shadow:none;
        }
    </style>

<article class="rbz-row" data-rbz-item data-rbz-population="규모 산정 기업" style="--rbz-accent:#2563eb" title="규모 산정 기업 기준 종합규모 상대위치">
