    <script>
        (function(){
            var script=document.currentScript, root=script?script.closest('[data-rbz]'):null;
            if(!root||root.dataset.ready==='Y') return;
            root.dataset.ready='Y';

            function n(v){
                if(v===null||v===undefined||String(v).trim()==='') return null;
                var x=Number(String(v).replace(/,/g,''));
                return Number.isFinite(x)?x:null;
            }
            function clamp(v,min,max){ return Math.min(max,Math.max(min,v)); }
            function pct(r,g,p){
                if(r!==null&&g!==null&&g>0) return r*100/g;
                return p!==null&&p>=0?p:null;
            }
            function fp(v){
                if(v===null) return '-';
                return v.toLocaleString('ko-KR',{maximumFractionDigits:v<.1?3:(v<10?2:1)});
            }
            function rankValue(v){
                if(v===null) return '-';
                if(v>0&&v<0.01) return '0.01% 이내';
                return '상위 '+fp(v)+'%';
            }
            function rankCenterText(v){
                return v!==null&&v>0&&v<0.01 ? '최상위' : '상위';
            }
            function fpAria(v){
                if(v===null) return '자료 없음';
                return v>0&&v<0.01 ? '최상위 0.01퍼센트 이내' : '상위 '+fp(v)+'퍼센트';
            }
            function fi(v){ return v===null?'-':Math.round(v).toLocaleString('ko-KR'); }
            function compact(v){
                if(v===null) return '-';
                if(v>=10000000) return (v/10000000).toLocaleString('ko-KR',{maximumFractionDigits:1})+'천만';
                if(v>=1000000) return (v/10000).toLocaleString('ko-KR',{maximumFractionDigits:0})+'만';
                if(v>=10000) return (v/10000).toLocaleString('ko-KR',{minimumFractionDigits:1,maximumFractionDigits:1})+'만';
                return fi(v);
            }
            function basis(v){
                if(v===null||v===undefined) return '-';
                var s=String(v).replace(/[^0-9]/g,'');
                if(s.length>=8) return s.slice(2,4)+'.'+s.slice(4,6)+'.'+s.slice(6,8);
                if(s.length>=6) return s.slice(2,4)+'.'+s.slice(4,6);
                return String(v);
            }
            function rankColor(p){
                if(p===null) return '#64748b';
                if(p<0.01) return '#be123c';
                if(p<=1) return '#dc2626';
                if(p<=10) return '#ea580c';
                if(p<=30) return '#0f766e';
                return '#475569';
            }
            function draw(chart){
                var r=n(chart.dataset.r), g=n(chart.dataset.g), p=pct(r,g,n(chart.dataset.p));
                if(p===null) return;
                var elite=p>0&&p<0.01;
                var score=100-clamp(p,0,100);
                var angle=Math.PI-(Math.PI*score/100);
                var cx=25+20*Math.cos(angle);
                var cy=24-20*Math.sin(angle);
                var item=chart.closest('[data-rbz-item]');
                var population=item&&item.dataset.rbzPopulation
                    ? item.dataset.rbzPopulation
                    : '해당 지표 산정 기업';
                var center=chart.querySelector('.rbz-arc-center');
                if(item){
                    item.style.setProperty('--rbz-rank-color',rankColor(p));
                    item.classList.toggle('is-elite',elite);
                }
                if(center){
                    center.textContent=rankCenterText(p);
                }
                var progress=chart.querySelector('[data-rbz-progress]');
                var marker=chart.querySelector('[data-rbz-marker]');
                if(progress){
                    var raf=window.requestAnimationFrame||function(cb){ return window.setTimeout(cb,0); };
                    progress.style.strokeDashoffset='100';
                    raf(function(){ progress.style.strokeDashoffset=String(100-score); });
                }
                if(marker){
                    marker.setAttribute('cx',cx.toFixed(2));
                    marker.setAttribute('cy',cy.toFixed(2));
                }
                chart.setAttribute('aria-label',population+' 기준 '+fpAria(p)+' 위치');
            }

            root.querySelectorAll('[data-rbz-rank]').forEach(function(el){
                var r=n(el.dataset.r), g=n(el.dataset.g), p=pct(r,g,n(el.dataset.p));
                var elite=p!==null&&p>0&&p<0.01;
                el.textContent=rankValue(p);
                el.classList.toggle('is-elite',elite);
                if(p!==null){
                    el.title=fpAria(p);
                }else{
                    el.removeAttribute('title');
                }
            });
            root.querySelectorAll('[data-rbz-exact]').forEach(function(el){
                var r=n(el.dataset.r), g=n(el.dataset.g);
                var item=el.closest('[data-rbz-item]');
                var population=item&&item.dataset.rbzPopulation
                    ? item.dataset.rbzPopulation
                    : '해당 지표 산정 기업';
                if(r===null||g===null){
                    el.textContent='-';
                    el.removeAttribute('title');
                    return;
                }
                el.textContent=fi(r)+'위 · '+compact(g)+'개 기준';
                el.title=population+' '+fi(g)+'개 중 '+fi(r)+'위';
            });
            root.querySelectorAll('[data-rbz-basis]').forEach(function(el){ el.textContent=basis(el.dataset.v); });
            root.querySelectorAll('[data-rbz-chart]').forEach(draw);

            var tabs=root.querySelectorAll('[data-rbz-tab]');
            var panes=root.querySelectorAll('[data-rbz-pane]');
            tabs.forEach(function(tab){
                tab.addEventListener('click',function(){
                    var key=tab.getAttribute('data-rbz-tab');
                    tabs.forEach(function(t){
                        var active=t===tab;
                        t.classList.toggle('is-active',active);
                        t.setAttribute('aria-selected',active?'true':'false');
                    });
                    panes.forEach(function(p){ p.hidden=p.getAttribute('data-rbz-pane')!==key; });
                });
            });
        })();
    </script>
</div>
                    <!-- 요약 기업 랭킹 포지션 끝 -->

             - Premium/Pro/Pro+/1일 Premium/기업 Premium 상세권한: 기존 실제 상세 동선 사용. -->

                    class="mbz-rank-insight-action only-pay-service"

                    <script>
                        function fn_show_stockInfo() {
                            if (typeof window.mbzActivateCompanyDetailSubTab !== 'function'
                                    || !window.mbzActivateCompanyDetailSubTab('content-tab-3')) {
                                return;
                            }

                            // 모바일 기기에서만 실행

                                }, 1000); // 1초 동안 부드럽게 이동

                    </script>
                    <div class="contentWrap shadow mbz-grade-sample-wrap mbz-grade-preview-wrap mbz-a8-shell" data-report-grade-boundary
                        style="border-radius: 10px; height: auto; position: relative; overflow: hidden;">
                        <input id="content-tab-1" class="content-tab" type="radio" name="nav" checked>
                        <input id="content-tab-2" class="content-tab" type="radio" name="nav">

            <!-- 공시·실적 인사이트 상세 시작 -->
            

    
    <script>
        window.MBZEarningsInsightSection = window.MBZEarningsInsightSection || {};

        /* company-earnings-insight.html 본문 연결/개별 기준 전환 */
        window.MBZEarningsInsightSection.changeBasis = function (btn) {
            if (!btn) return false;

            var fsDiv = btn.getAttribute('data-fs-div') || '';
            if (fsDiv !== 'CFS' && fsDiv !== 'OFS') return false;

            if (btn.classList && btn.classList.contains('is-active')) {
                return false;
            }

            try {
                if (btn.classList) {
                    btn.classList.add('is-loading');
                }

                var url = new URL(window.location.href);
                url.searchParams.set('fsDivInSight', fsDiv);
                url.hash = 'mbzSectionEarningsInsight';

                window.location.href = url.toString();
            } catch (e) {
                var sep = window.location.href.indexOf('?') >= 0 ? '&' : '?';
                window.location.href = window.location.href.split('#')[0]
                        + sep
                        + 'fsDivInSight='
                        + encodeURIComponent(fsDiv)
                        + '#mbzSectionEarningsInsight';
            }

            return false;
        };
    </script>

    <style>
        .mbz-report-th-wrap{ display:flex; flex-direction:column; gap:6px; font-size:12px; line-height:1.2; }
        .mbz-report-th-title{ font-weight:700; font-size:12.5px; line-height:1.1; color:inherit; }
        .mbz-report-th-meta{ font-size:11.5px; color:inherit; opacity:.92; margin-top:2px; }
        .mbz-report-th-meta .num{ font-weight:800; color:white;}
        .mbz-report-th-status{
          display:inline-flex; align-items:center; gap:4px; width:max-content; max-width:100%;
          margin-top:5px; padding:3px 7px; border:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.58); border-radius:9999px;
          background:rgba(255,255,255,.18); color:#fff7ed; font-size:10px; font-weight:900; line-height:1.2;
          white-space:nowrap;
        }
        .mbz-report-th-status i{ font-size:9px; }

        .mbz-report-th-promo{ margin-top:6px; display:flex; justify-content:center; }

        /* ⬇️ (중요) 기존 160px/64x78 → 더 작게 */
        .mbz-report-sample-btn{
          border:0; width:150px; max-width:100%;
          padding:12px 10px 10px; border-radius:16px;
          background:rgba(255,255,255,.55);
          cursor:pointer;
        }
        .mbz-report-sample-btn:active{ transform:translateY(1px); }

        .mbz-report-sample-inner{ display:flex; flex-direction:column; align-items:center; gap:8px; }
        .mbz-report-sample-svg{ width:56px; height:68px; display:block; }

        .mbz-report-sample-caption{
          text-align:center; font-weight:900; font-size:11px; line-height:1.05;
          color:rgba(15,23,42,.55);
          font-family:-apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,"Segoe UI",system-ui,sans-serif;
          -webkit-font-smoothing:antialiased;
          text-rendering:optimizeLegibility;
        }

        @media (max-width:768px){
          .mbz-report-sample-btn{ width:138px; padding:11px 8px 9px; }
          .mbz-report-sample-svg{ width:52px; height:63px; }
          .mbz-report-sample-caption{ font-size:10.5px; }

    <style>
        .mbz-sic-wrapper { max-width:100%; box-sizing:border-box; }
        .mbz-sic-list{
          display:flex; flex-direction:column; gap:4px;
          overflow-y:auto; padding-right:6px;
        }
        .mbz-sic-item{
          display:flex; align-items:center; gap:8px;
          border-radius:10px; border:1px solid #e5e7eb;
          padding:4px 8px; min-width:0;
        }
        .mbz-sic-code{
          flex:0 0 auto; font-size:11px; padding:2px 8px; border-radius:9999px;
          color:#334155; white-space:nowrap;
        }
        .mbz-sic-name{
          flex:1 1 auto; min-width:0; font-size:12px; color:#0f172a;
          overflow:hidden; text-overflow:ellipsis; white-space:nowrap;
        }
        .mbz-sic-report-badge{
          flex:0 0 auto; display:inline-flex; align-items:center; gap:4px;
          padding:3px 8px; border-radius:9999px; border:none;
          background:#e5edff; color:#4338ca; font-size:11px; cursor:pointer;
          white-space:nowrap;
        }
        .mbz-sic-report-badge:hover{ background:#d8f7ef; color:#067b70; }
        .mbz-sic-report-badge:focus-visible{ outline:2px solid #0f9f8f; outline-offset:2px; }
        body.darkmode .mbz-sic-report-badge,body[data-theme="dark"] .mbz-sic-report-badge{ background:rgba(15,159,143,.15); color:#7ee8d9; }
        .mbz-sic-empty{
          display:none; padding:10px 12px; border-radius:12px;
          border:1px dashed #cbd5e1; background:#f8fafc; color:#64748b; font-size:12px;
        }
        .mbz-sic-sync-notice{
          margin-top:7px; padding:8px 10px; border:1px solid #fdba74; border-radius:10px;
          background:#fff7ed; color:#7c2d12; box-sizing:border-box;
        }
        .mbz-sic-sync-notice__head{
          display:flex; align-items:center; flex-wrap:wrap; gap:6px; margin-bottom:3px;
          font-size:11px; line-height:1.2;
        }
        .mbz-sic-sync-notice__badge{
          display:inline-flex; align-items:center; gap:4px; min-height:20px; padding:2px 7px;
          border:1px solid #fdba74; border-radius:9999px; background:#ffedd5; color:#c2410c;
          font-size:10px; font-weight:900; white-space:nowrap;
        }
        .mbz-sic-sync-notice__head strong{ color:#9a3412; font-weight:950; }
        .mbz-sic-sync-notice p{
          margin:0; color:#7c2d12; font-size:11px; font-weight:750; line-height:1.45; word-break:keep-all;
        }
        .mbz-sic-sync-notice p strong{ font-weight:950; color:#9a3412; }
        .mbz-sic-code-lock{
          display:flex; align-items:center; justify-content:space-between; gap:10px;
          margin-top:8px; padding:8px 10px; border:1px dashed #bbf7d0;
          border-radius:10px; background:#f0fdf4; color:#15803d; font-size:11px;
        }
        .mbz-sic-code-lock__copy{min-width:0; line-height:1.45; word-break:keep-all;}
        .mbz-sic-code-lock__cta{
          flex:0 0 auto; min-height:28px; padding:5px 9px; border:1px solid #86efac;
          border-radius:8px; background:#fff; color:#16a34a; font-size:10.5px; font-weight:900;
          white-space:nowrap; cursor:pointer;
        }
        body.darkmode .mbz-sic-sync-notice,
        body[data-theme="dark"] .mbz-sic-sync-notice{
          border-color:rgba(251,146,60,.42); background:rgba(124,45,18,.18); color:#fed7aa;
        }
        body.darkmode .mbz-sic-sync-notice__badge,
        body[data-theme="dark"] .mbz-sic-sync-notice__badge{
          border-color:rgba(251,146,60,.42); background:rgba(154,52,18,.28); color:#fdba74;
        }
        body.darkmode .mbz-sic-sync-notice__head strong,
        body[data-theme="dark"] .mbz-sic-sync-notice__head strong,
        body.darkmode .mbz-sic-sync-notice p,
        body[data-theme="dark"] .mbz-sic-sync-notice p,
        body.darkmode .mbz-sic-sync-notice p strong,
        body[data-theme="dark"] .mbz-sic-sync-notice p strong{
          color:#fed7aa;
        }
        body.darkmode .mbz-sic-code-lock,
        body[data-theme="dark"] .mbz-sic-code-lock{
          border-color:rgba(74,222,128,.42); background:rgba(22,163,74,.15); color:#86efac;
        }
        body.darkmode .mbz-sic-code-lock__cta,
        body[data-theme="dark"] .mbz-sic-code-lock__cta{
          border-color:rgba(134,239,172,.45); background:#25283a; color:#86efac;
        }

        /* iframe 모달 */
        .mbz-sic-modal{ position:fixed; inset:0; z-index:9999; display:none; }
        .mbz-sic-modal.is-open{ display:block; }
        .mbz-sic-modal-backdrop{ position:absolute; inset:0; background:rgba(2,6,23,0.72); }
        .mbz-sic-modal-panel{
          position:absolute; inset:24px; border-radius:16px; overflow:hidden;
          background:#0b1220; box-shadow:0 24px 60px rgba(0,0,0,0.55);
          display:flex; flex-direction:column;
        }
        .mbz-sic-modal-topbar{
          display:flex; align-items:center; justify-content:space-between;
          padding:10px 12px; background:#0f172a; border-bottom:1px solid #1f2937;
          color:#e5e7eb; font-size:13px;
        }
        .mbz-sic-modal-close{
          border:none; background:transparent; color:#9ca3af; font-size:20px;
          cursor:pointer; padding:2px 8px;
        }
        .mbz-sic-modal-close:hover{ color:#e5e7eb; }
        .mbz-sic-modal-iframe{ width:100%; height:100%; border:none; background:#020617; }

        @media (max-width:768px){
          .mbz-sic-modal-panel{ inset:10px; }
          .mbz-sic-name{ white-space:normal; overflow:visible; text-overflow:clip; }
          .mbz-sic-sync-notice{ padding:8px 9px; }
          .mbz-sic-sync-notice__head{ gap:5px; }
        }
    </style>

    <!-- 일반(전체 기능) -->
    <div class="mbz-sic-wrapper" id="mbz-industry-report-entry" data-industry-report-entry="6168628898">
        <div hidden aria-hidden="true" data-industry-report-ready-sics>
            
        </div>
        <div class="mbz-sic-empty" id="mbz-sic-empty-6168628898">산업분류 정보가 없습니다.</div>
        <div class="mbz-sic-list" id="mbz-sic-list-6168628898"></div>

        

        <div class="mbz-sic-code-lock" data-nosnippet>
            <span class="mbz-sic-code-lock__copy">업종코드는 Standard부터 확인할 수 있습니다.</span>
            <button type="button"
                    class="mbz-sic-code-lock__cta only-pay-service"
                    data-id="AS002"
                    aria-label="Standard에서 산업분류 코드 확인">
                업종코드 확인
            </button>
        </div>
    </div>

    <script>
        /*<![CDATA[*/
        (function () {

          var bno = "6168628898";
          var raw = "\uC2DC\uC2A4\uD15C \uC18C\uD504\uD2B8\uC6E8\uC5B4 \uAC1C\uBC1C \uBC0F \uACF5\uAE09\uC5C5";

          raw = (raw == null) ? '' : String(raw);

          var listEl  = document.getElementById('mbz-sic-list-' + bno);
          var emptyEl = document.getElementById('mbz-sic-empty-' + bno);
          var rcntEl  = document.getElementById('mbz-sic-report-count-' + bno);
          var entryEl = document.querySelector('[data-industry-report-entry="' + bno + '"]');
          var reportReadyBySic = Object.create(null);

          if (entryEl) {
            var readyNodes = entryEl.querySelectorAll('[data-industry-report-ready-sic]');
            for (var readyIdx = 0; readyIdx < readyNodes.length; readyIdx++) {
              var readyCode = String(readyNodes[readyIdx].getAttribute('data-industry-report-ready-sic') || '')
                .replace(/[^A-Za-z0-9]/g, '').toUpperCase();
              if (readyCode) reportReadyBySic[readyCode] = true;
            }
          }

          if (!listEl) return;

          // ---------- parse ----------
          function parseLines(text) {
            if (!text) return [];
            var t = String(text)
              .replace(/<br\s*\/?>/gi, '\n')
              .replace(/\r\n/g, '\n')
              .replace(/\r/g, '\n');

            // trim + empty 제거
            var arr = t.split('\n');
            var out = [];
            for (var i = 0; i < arr.length; i++) {
              var s = arr[i].trim();
              if (s) out.push(s);
            }
            return out;
          }

          var codeRe = /^\(([^)]+)\)\s*(.*)$/;

          function splitCodeAndName(line) {
            var m = codeRe.exec(line);
            if (m) return { code: (m[1] || '').trim(), name: (m[2] || '').trim() };
            return { code: '', name: line };
          }

          // ---------- render (즉시) ----------
          var lines = parseLines(raw);

          if (rcntEl) rcntEl.textContent = String(lines.length); // 현재는 라인=보고서 제공 개수 가정

          listEl.innerHTML = '';

          if (!lines.length) {
            if (emptyEl) emptyEl.style.display = 'block';
            return;
          }
          if (emptyEl) emptyEl.style.display = 'none';

          var frag = document.createDocumentFragment();

          for (var j = 0; j < lines.length; j++) {
            var line = lines[j];
            var p = splitCodeAndName(line);

            var item = document.createElement('div');
            item.className = 'mbz-sic-item' + (p.code ? '' : ' is-name-only');

            if (p.code) {
              var code = document.createElement('span');
              code.className = 'mbz-sic-code';
              code.textContent = p.code;
              item.appendChild(code);
            }

            var name = document.createElement('span');
            name.className = 'mbz-sic-name';
            name.textContent = p.name || line;
            item.appendChild(name);

            var isSicRow = !!p.code;
            var normalizedSicCode = String(p.code || '').replace(/[^A-Za-z0-9]/g, '').toUpperCase();
            var reportReady = !!reportReadyBySic[normalizedSicCode];
            if (isSicRow && reportReady) {
              var btn = document.createElement('button');
              btn.type = 'button'; btn.className = 'mbz-sic-report-badge';
              btn.title = '산업구조·동종기업 벤치마크 보기';
              btn.setAttribute('aria-label', (p.name || p.code) + ' 산업구조·동종기업 벤치마크');
              btn.dataset.bno=bno; btn.dataset.sicCode=p.code||''; btn.dataset.sicLine=line; btn.dataset.sicName=p.name||'';
              var icon=document.createElement('i'); icon.className='fa fa-bar-chart'; icon.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');
              var label=document.createElement('span'); label.textContent='벤치마크';
              btn.appendChild(icon); btn.appendChild(label); item.appendChild(btn);
            }
            frag.appendChild(item);
          }

          listEl.appendChild(frag);

          // ---------- modal (한 번만 생성) ----------
          function ensureModal() {
            var modal = document.getElementById('mbz-sic-report-modal');
            if (modal) return modal;

            modal = document.createElement('div');
            modal.id = 'mbz-sic-report-modal';
            modal.className = 'mbz-sic-modal';
            modal.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');

            modal.innerHTML =
              '<div class="mbz-sic-modal-backdrop" data-sic-modal-close="1"></div>' +
              '<div class="mbz-sic-modal-panel" role="dialog" aria-modal="true">' +
              '  <div class="mbz-sic-modal-topbar">' +
              '    <div id="mbz-sic-report-modal-title">산업구조·동종기업 벤치마크</div>' +
              '    <button type="button" class="mbz-sic-modal-close" title="닫기" data-sic-modal-close="1">×</button>' +
              '  </div>' +
              '  <div class="mbz-sic-modal-loading" id="mbz-sic-loading">불러오는 중...</div>' +
              '  <iframe id="mbz-sic-report-iframe" class="mbz-sic-modal-iframe" src="about:blank"></iframe>' +
              '</div>';

            document.body.appendChild(modal);

            // 닫기
            modal.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
              var t = e.target;
              if (t && t.getAttribute && t.getAttribute('data-sic-modal-close') === '1') {
                e.preventDefault();
                closeModal();
              }
            });

            document.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) {
              if (e.key === 'Escape') closeModal();
            });

            // iframe 로딩 표시/해제
            var iframe = document.getElementById('mbz-sic-report-iframe');
            if (iframe) {
              iframe.addEventListener('load', function () {
                var loading = document.getElementById('mbz-sic-loading');
                if (loading) loading.style.display = 'none';
              });
            }

            return modal;
          }

          function openModal(url, title) {
            var modal = ensureModal();

            var titleEl = document.getElementById('mbz-sic-report-modal-title');
            if (titleEl && title) titleEl.textContent = title;

            var loading = document.getElementById('mbz-sic-loading');
            if (loading) loading.style.display = 'flex';

            // 먼저 모달을 "즉시" 띄우고(체감 속도 개선)
            modal.classList.add('is-open');
            modal.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'false');
            document.documentElement.classList.add('mbz-sic-modal-open');
            document.body.classList.add('mbz-sic-modal-open');

            // 다음 프레임에 iframe src 설정(첫 페인트 보장)
            requestAnimationFrame(function () {
              var iframe = document.getElementById('mbz-sic-report-iframe');
              if (iframe) iframe.src = url;
            });
          }

          function closeModal() {
            var modal = document.getElementById('mbz-sic-report-modal');
            if (!modal) return;

            modal.classList.remove('is-open');
            modal.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');
            document.documentElement.classList.remove('mbz-sic-modal-open');
            document.body.classList.remove('mbz-sic-modal-open');

            var iframe = document.getElementById('mbz-sic-report-iframe');
            if (iframe) iframe.src = 'about:blank';
          }

          // 외부(iframe)에서 닫기 호출
          window.closeSicReportSample = window.closeSicReportSample || closeModal;
          window.closeSicReport = window.closeSicReport || closeModal;

          // 산업분류 보고서 샘플 기능은 아직 미개발 상태이므로 호출 진입점을 비활성화한다.
          // 샘플 기능 개발/공개 시 실제 Controller 경로를 확정한 뒤 함께 활성화할 것.
          /*
          window.openSicReportSample = window.openSicReportSample || function () {
            openModal('/report/sic/sample', '산업구조·동종기업 벤치마크 샘플');
          };
          */

          function requireIndustryLogin(){if(typeof window.mbzLoginPop==='function')return window.mbzLoginPop();if(typeof window.fn_login==='function')return window.fn_login();window.location.href='/loginPage?redirectUrl='+encodeURIComponent(window.location.href);return false;}
          function openIndustryPlan(){if(typeof window.mbzOpenUpgradeBridgeOrPay==='function')return window.mbzOpenUpgradeBridgeOrPay('AS007');if(typeof window.fn_joinProd==='function')return window.fn_joinProd('AS007');window.location.href='/goodsPlan';return false;}
          function openIndustryBenchmarkWithAccess(bnoVal,sicCode,sicName){
            if(!bnoVal||!sicCode)return false;
            fetch('/api/company/'+encodeURIComponent(bnoVal)+'/analysis-report-hub',{method:'GET',credentials:'same-origin',cache:'no-store',headers:{'Accept':'application/json','X-Requested-With':'XMLHttpRequest'}})
              .then(function(response){if(response.status===401){requireIndustryLogin();return null;}if(!response.ok)throw new Error('INDUSTRY_HUB_'+response.status);return response.json();})
              .then(function(data){if(!data)return;var card=null;(Array.isArray(data.cards)?data.cards:[]).some(function(c){if(String(c&&c.key||'').toUpperCase()==='INDUSTRY'){card=c;return true;}return false;});if(!card){window.alert('산업구조·동종기업 벤치마크 상태를 확인할 수 없습니다.');return;}var action=String(card.actionType||'').toUpperCase();var access=String(card.accessCode||'').toUpperCase();if(action==='LOGIN')return requireIndustryLogin();if(action==='PLAN')return openIndustryPlan();if(access==='PRO_VIEW_QUOTA_EXHAUSTED'){if(typeof window.showMbzReportQuotaExceeded==='function'){window.showMbzReportQuotaExceeded({bNo:bnoVal,reportType:'INDUSTRY',usageType:'REPORT_VIEW'});return;}window.alert('이번 달 정식 보고서 열람 한도를 모두 사용했습니다.');return;}if(action==='PREVIEW'||action==='PRODUCT'){if(window.MBZAnalysisReportHub&&typeof window.MBZAnalysisReportHub.openReportPreview==='function')return window.MBZAnalysisReportHub.openReportPreview('INDUSTRY',bnoVal,card&&card.title);if(window.MBZAnalysisReportHub&&typeof window.MBZAnalysisReportHub.openIndustryProductGuide==='function')return window.MBZAnalysisReportHub.openIndustryProductGuide(bnoVal,card);window.location.href='/companyDetail/'+encodeURIComponent(bnoVal)+'#mbz-report-hub';return;}if(action!=='SCROLL'&&action!=='OPEN'){window.alert(card.statusText||'현재 산업구조·동종기업 벤치마크를 열 수 없습니다.');return;}if(typeof window.openMbzIndustryBenchmarkReport==='function')return window.openMbzIndustryBenchmarkReport(bnoVal,sicCode,sicName||'');if(typeof window.openIndustryReport==='function')return window.openIndustryReport(bnoVal,sicCode,sicName||'');window.location.href='/'+encodeURIComponent(bnoVal)+'/'+encodeURIComponent(sicCode)+'/industryReport';})
              .catch(function(error){if(window.console&&console.error)console.error('[INDUSTRY_REPORT_PREFLIGHT_FAIL]',error);window.alert('보고서 이용권을 확인하지 못했습니다. 잠시 후 다시 시도해 주세요.');});
            return false;
          }

          // ---------- 클릭 바인딩 (중복 방지) ----------
          // 컨테이너(listEl)에만 한번 바인딩: 매우 빠르고 중복 없음
          if (!listEl.__sicBound) {
            listEl.__sicBound = true;
            listEl.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
              var target = e.target;
              if (!target) return;

              // 버튼 내부의 i/span 클릭도 처리
              var btn = target.closest ? target.closest('.mbz-sic-report-badge') : null;
              if (!btn) return;

              e.preventDefault();
              var bnoVal = btn.getAttribute('data-bno');
              var sicCode = btn.getAttribute('data-sic-code');
              var sicLine = btn.getAttribute('data-sic-line');

              var sicName = btn.getAttribute('data-sic-name') || '';
              openIndustryBenchmarkWithAccess(bnoVal, sicCode, sicName);
            });
          }
        })();
        /*]]>*/
    </script>


                            <div class="mbz-industry-more-free">
                                <span class="mbz-industry-more-free__text">등록 업종 2개 중 1개 미리보기</span>
                                <button type="button"
                                        class="mbz-access-cta mbz-access-cta--free mbz-free-view-btn mbz-industry-more-free__cta only-free-service"
                                        aria-label="로그인 또는 무료회원 가입 후 전체 등록 산업분류 보기">
                                    <span>무료로 전체 업종명 보기</span>
                                    <span class="mbz-free-view-arrow" aria-hidden="true">›</span>
                                </button>
                            </div>

                            <!-- MBZ M3.4 - 모바일 Free 회원은 중복 잠금카드 대신 단일 단계 브리지로 표시한다. -->
                            

                            <div class="mgt5 mbz-industry-weight-teaser mbz-industry-weight-teaser-free"
                                 style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:minmax(260px,1fr) 520px;align-items:stretch;gap:14px;padding:12px 14px;border:1px solid #fed7aa;border-radius:14px;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#fff7ed 0%,#fff 78%);box-shadow:0 8px 20px rgba(249,115,22,.10);">
                                <div class="mbz-industry-lock-copy" style="min-width:0;display:flex;flex-direction:column;justify-content:flex-start;align-items:flex-start;min-height:104px;padding-top:4px;">
                                    <span style="align-self:flex-start;display:inline-flex;align-items:center;width:auto;max-width:max-content;height:18px;padding:0 7px;border-radius:999px;background:#fff7ed;border:1px solid #fdba74;color:#c2410c;font-size:10px;font-weight:950;line-height:1;white-space:nowrap;">
                                        PREMIUM INSIGHT
                                    </span>
                                    <b class="mbz-industry-lock-title" style="display:block;margin-top:5px;color:#111827;font-size:13px;line-height:1.35;">
                                        동종비교 업종 가중치
                                    </b>
                                    <span class="mbz-industry-lock-desc" style="display:block;margin-top:4px;color:#64748b;font-size:11px;font-weight:750;line-height:1.45;">
                                        공시·사업부문 등을 바탕으로 산정한 동종비교 참고 비중과 근거를 확인합니다.
                                    </span>
                                </div>

                                <div class="mbz-industry-lock-layout" style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:minmax(300px,1fr) 160px;align-items:stretch;gap:12px;min-width:0;">
                                    <div class="mbz-industry-lock-preview" style="position:relative;min-height:104px;height:100%;padding:10px 12px;border:1px solid #fed7aa;border-radius:16px;background:rgba(255,255,255,.90);overflow:hidden;">
                                        <div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:94px minmax(0,1fr);align-items:center;gap:14px;filter:blur(2.2px);opacity:.58;pointer-events:none;user-select:none;" aria-hidden="true">
                                            <div style="position:relative;width:86px;height:86px;border-radius:999px;background:conic-gradient(#f97316 0 58%,#2563eb 58% 88%,#16a34a 88% 100%);box-shadow:inset 0 0 0 2px rgba(255,255,255,.94),0 10px 20px rgba(15,23,42,.10);">
                                                <span style="position:absolute;inset:25px;border-radius:999px;background:#fff;box-shadow:inset 0 0 0 1px #e5e7eb;"></span>
                                                <span style="position:absolute;z-index:1;inset:0;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;color:#111827;font-size:10.5px;font-weight:950;text-align:center;line-height:1.18;">3개<br>업종</span>
                                            </div>

                                            <div style="display:grid;gap:6px;min-width:0;">
                                                <div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:10px minmax(0,1fr) 40px;align-items:center;gap:7px;">
                                                    <span style="width:10px;height:10px;border-radius:999px;background:#f97316;"></span>
                                                    <span style="height:9px;border-radius:999px;background:#e5e7eb;"></span>
                                                    <span style="height:9px;border-radius:999px;background:#e5e7eb;"></span>
                                                </div>
                                                <div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:10px minmax(0,1fr) 40px;align-items:center;gap:7px;">
                                                    <span style="width:10px;height:10px;border-radius:999px;background:#2563eb;"></span>
                                                    <span style="height:9px;border-radius:999px;background:#e5e7eb;"></span>
                                                    <span style="height:9px;border-radius:999px;background:#e5e7eb;"></span>
                                                </div>
                                                <div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:10px minmax(0,1fr) 40px;align-items:center;gap:7px;">
                                                    <span style="width:10px;height:10px;border-radius:999px;background:#16a34a;"></span>
                                                    <span style="height:9px;border-radius:999px;background:#e5e7eb;"></span>
                                                    <span style="height:9px;border-radius:999px;background:#e5e7eb;"></span>
                                                </div>

            <script>
                (function () {
                    const freeTabCounts = {
                        '2': 1,
                        '3': 0,
                        '4': 0,
                        '5': 0
                    };

                    function renderFreeLockedTab(seq, panel) {
                        if (!panel) return;
                        if (panel.getAttribute('data-loaded') === 'true') return;

                        const tabCount = Number(freeTabCounts[String(seq)] || 0);
                        panel.setAttribute('data-loaded', 'true');

                        if (tabCount > 0) {
                            panel.innerHTML =
                                "<table class='mbz-table'>"
                                + "    <tr class='mbz-tab-tr'>"
                                + "        <td class='mbz-tab-td red strong italic' colspan='4'>"
                                + "            <em class='only-pay-service hands mbz-wave-cta mbz-access-cta mbz-access-cta--standard is-standard' data-id='AS002'>"
                                + "                스탠다드에서 보기"
                                + "            </em>"
                                + "        </td>"
                                + "    </tr>"
                                + "</table>";
                        } else {
                            panel.innerHTML =
                                "<table class='mbz-table'>"
                                + "    <tr class='mbz-tab-tr'>"
                                + "        <td class='mbz-tab-td' colspan='4'>조회 된 내용이 없습니다.</td>"
                                + "    </tr>"
                                + "</table>";
                        }
                    }

                    function setActiveCeoEtcTab(seq) {
                        document.querySelectorAll('.ceoEtcTabBtn').forEach(function (btn) {
                            const active = btn.getAttribute('data-seq') === String(seq);
                            btn.classList.toggle('active', active);
                            btn.setAttribute('aria-selected', active ? 'true' : 'false');
                        });

                        document.querySelectorAll('.ceoEtcIbContArea').forEach(function (panel) {
                            panel.classList.add('hide');
                            panel.style.display = 'none';
                            panel.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');
                        });

                        const targetPanel = document.getElementById('ceoEtcTabcont' + seq);
                        if (!targetPanel) return;

                        targetPanel.classList.remove('hide');
                        targetPanel.style.display = 'block';
                        targetPanel.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'false');

                        if (String(seq) !== '1') {
                            renderFreeLockedTab(seq, targetPanel);
                        }
                    }

                    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {
                        document.querySelectorAll('.ceoEtcTabBtn').forEach(function (btn) {
                            btn.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
                                e.preventDefault();
                                const seq = this.getAttribute('data-seq');
                                setActiveCeoEtcTab(seq);
                            });
                        });

                        setActiveCeoEtcTab('1');
                    });

                    window.activateCeoEtcTab = setActiveCeoEtcTab;
                })();
            </script>

                         data-scroll-target="mbzDetailTabAnchor">기업 HR 분석
                    </div>
                    <div class="mbz-qk-anchor mbz-quick-menu"
                         data-major-word="유사/이웃"
                         data-qk-title="AI 비교후보/이웃업체"
                         data-qk-short="유사/이웃"
                         data-activate-target="mbzMainPanelNeighbor"
                         data-scroll-target="mbzDetailTabAnchor">AI 비교후보/이웃업체
                    </div>
                    
                    
                    
                    
                    
                    
                </div>
            <script>
                (function () {
                    function initMarketbizDetailTabs() {
                        var hub = document.getElementById('mbzDetailTabAnchor');
                        if (!hub) {
                            return;
                        }

                        function hasMeaningfulContent(node) {
                            if (!node) {
                                return false;
                            }
                            var text = (node.textContent || '').replace(/\s+/g, '');
                            if (text.length > 8) {
                                return true;
                            }
                            return !!node.querySelector('table,img,picture,svg,canvas,iframe,.grid-title,.kiscon-summary-wrap,.mbz-companyscale-index-container');
                        }

                        function refreshPanels(target) {
                            var activePanel = target
                                ? document.getElementById(target)
                                : hub.querySelector('.mbz-main-panel.is-active');
                            if (!activePanel) return;

                            window.requestAnimationFrame(function () {
                                try {
                                    if (window.Highcharts && Array.isArray(window.Highcharts.charts)) {
                                        window.Highcharts.charts.forEach(function (chart) {
                                            var renderTo = chart && chart.renderTo;
                                            if (renderTo && activePanel.contains(renderTo)
                                                    && typeof chart.reflow === 'function') {
                                                chart.reflow();
                                            }
                                        });
                                    }
                                } catch (e) {
                                }
                            });
                        }

                        function activateTab(target) {
                            ensureMountedPanel(target);

                            hub.querySelectorAll('.mbz-main-tab-btn').forEach(function (btn) {
                                var active = btn.getAttribute('data-target') === target;
                                btn.classList.toggle('is-active', active);
                                btn.classList.toggle('active', active);
                                btn.setAttribute('aria-selected', active ? 'true' : 'false');
                            });

                            hub.querySelectorAll('.mbz-main-panel').forEach(function (panel) {
                                var active = panel.id === target;
                                panel.classList.toggle('is-active', active);
                                panel.style.display = active ? 'block' : 'none';
                                panel.setAttribute('aria-hidden', active ? 'false' : 'true');

                                panel.querySelectorAll('.mbz-mounted-block').forEach(function (mounted) {
                                    mounted.style.display = active ? 'block' : 'none';
                                });
                            });

                            if (target === 'mbzMainPanelMfdsRisk' && typeof window.mbzLoadMfdsRiskDetailAjax === 'function') {
                                window.setTimeout(window.mbzLoadMfdsRiskDetailAjax, 0);
                            }

                            try {
                                window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('marketbiz:hub-tab-change', {
                                    detail: { target: target }
                                }));
                            } catch (e) {}

                            setTimeout(function () { refreshPanels(target); }, 80);
                        }

                        window.marketbizDetailHubActivateTab = activateTab;

                        function hideTab(target) {
                            var btn = hub.querySelector('.mbz-main-tab-btn[data-target="' + target + '"]');
                            if (btn) {
                                var item = btn.closest('.nav-item');
                                if (item) {
                                    item.remove();
                                }
                            }
                            var panel = document.getElementById(target);
                            if (panel) {
                                panel.remove();
                            }
                        }

                        function renumberTabs() {
                            var seq = 1;
                            hub.querySelectorAll('.mbz-main-tab-btn .mbz-main-tab-badge').forEach(function (badge) {
                                badge.textContent = String(seq).padStart(2, '0');
                                seq += 1;
                            });
                        }

                        function suppressSpecialLeadHeading(source) {
                            if (!source) {
                                return;
                            }
                            var keywords = /(병|의원|학교|교육|건설|수행역량|자격|리스크)/;
                            var candidates = source.querySelectorAll('.grid-title,.mbz-panel-title,h1,h2,h3,.title');
                            for (var i = 0; i < candidates.length; i += 1) {
                                var el = candidates[i];
                                var txt = (el.textContent || '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
                                if (!txt || !keywords.test(txt)) {
                                    continue;
                                }
                                if (el.closest('.nav,.mbz-main-tabs-wrap,.d4-tab,.mbz-main-tabs')) {
                                    continue;
                                }
                                var target = el.closest('.grid-title,.mbz-panel-title') || el;
                                target.style.display = 'none';
                                break;
                            }
                        }

                        function mountSource(sourceId, panelId, role) {
                            var panel = document.getElementById(panelId);
                            if (!panel) return false;

                            var source = document.getElementById(sourceId);
                            if (!source) return false;

                            /* R36: already-mounted tabs skip full subtree textContent scan on repeated clicks. */
                            if (source.parentNode === panel && source.getAttribute('data-mbz-mounted-source') === sourceId) {
                                return true;
                            }

                            if (!hasMeaningfulContent(source)) return false;

                            var alreadyMounted = panel.querySelector('[data-mbz-mounted-source="' + sourceId + '"]');
                            if (alreadyMounted) return true;

                            function markMounted() {
                                source.classList.remove('mbz-source-stash');
                                source.classList.add('mbz-mounted-block');
                                source.style.display = 'block';

                                if (role) {
                                    source.setAttribute('data-mbz-role', role);
                                }

                                source.setAttribute('data-mbz-mounted-source', sourceId);

                                if (role === 'special' && !/Construction|StartupCommerce/.test(sourceId)) {
                                    suppressSpecialLeadHeading(source);
                                }
                            }

                            /*
                             * 방어 1:
                             * source가 이미 panel 안에 들어가 있으면 다시 append 하지 않는다.
                             */
                            if (panel.contains(source)) {
                                markMounted();
                                return true;
                            }

                            /*
                             * 방어 2:
                             * source가 panel의 부모/조상인 경우 appendChild 하면 HierarchyRequestError 발생.
                             * 이 경우 source를 화면에 노출시키면 원래 자리에서 깨져 보이므로
                             * mounted 처리하지 않고 실패로 반환한다.
                             */
                            if (source.contains(panel)) {
                                console.warn('mountSource skipped because source contains target panel', {
                                    sourceId: sourceId,
                                    panelId: panelId
                                });
                                return false;
                            }

                            panel.querySelectorAll('.mbz-panel-empty').forEach(function (el) {
                                el.remove();
                            });

                            markMounted();

                            try {
                                panel.appendChild(source);
                            } catch (e) {
                                console.warn('mountSource skipped because DOM hierarchy is invalid', {
                                    sourceId: sourceId,
                                    panelId: panelId,
                                    error: e
                                });
                                return false;
                            }

                            return true;
                        }

                        var panelSourceMap = {
                            mbzMainPanelOverview: { sourceId: 'mbzProductSectionSource', role: 'product' },
                            mbzMainPanelRank: { sourceId: 'mbzRankSectionSource', role: 'rank' },
                            mbzMainPanelHr: { sourceId: 'mbzHrSectionSource', role: 'hr' },
                            mbzMainPanelNeighbor: { sourceId: 'mbzNeighborSectionSource', role: 'neighbor' },
                            mbzMainPanelMfdsRisk: { sourceId: 'mbzMfdsRiskSectionSource', role: 'risk' },
                            mbzMainPanelConstruction: { sourceId: 'mbzSpecialConstructionWorkspaceSource', role: 'special' },
                            mbzMainPanelStartupCommerce: { sourceId: 'mbzStartupCommerceSectionSource', role: 'special' },
                            mbzMainPanelHospital: { sourceId: 'mbzSpecialHospitalSource', role: 'special' },
                            mbzMainPanelSchool: { sourceId: 'mbzSpecialSchoolSource', role: 'special' },
                            mbzMainPanelEducationInsight: { sourceId: 'mbzSpecialEducationInsightSource', role: 'special' }
                        };

                        function sourceAvailable(panelId) {
                            var cfg = panelSourceMap[panelId];

                            if (!cfg) {
                                return !!document.getElementById(panelId);
                            }

                            var source = document.getElementById(cfg.sourceId);
                            return !!(source && hasMeaningfulContent(source));
                        }

                        function ensureMountedPanel(panelId) {
                            var cfg = panelSourceMap[panelId];

                            if (!cfg) {
                                return true;
                            }

                            return mountSource(cfg.sourceId, panelId, cfg.role);
                        }

                        function mountAllDetailHubPanels() {
                            Object.keys(panelSourceMap).forEach(function (panelId) {
                                if (document.getElementById(panelId)) {
                                    ensureMountedPanel(panelId);
                                }
                            });

                            if (document.getElementById('mbzMfdsRiskAjaxRoot')
                                && typeof window.mbzLoadMfdsRiskDetailAjax === 'function') {
                                window.setTimeout(window.mbzLoadMfdsRiskDetailAjax, 0);
                            }

                            window.setTimeout(function () { refreshPanels(); }, 80);
                            return true;
                        }

                        window.marketbizDetailHubMountAllPanels = mountAllDetailHubPanels;

                        var status = {
                            overview: sourceAvailable('mbzMainPanelOverview'),
                            rank: sourceAvailable('mbzMainPanelRank'),
                            hr: sourceAvailable('mbzMainPanelHr'),
                            neighbor: sourceAvailable('mbzMainPanelNeighbor'),
                            mfdsRisk: sourceAvailable('mbzMainPanelMfdsRisk'),
                            construction: sourceAvailable('mbzMainPanelConstruction'),
                            startupCommerce: sourceAvailable('mbzMainPanelStartupCommerce'),
                            hospital: sourceAvailable('mbzMainPanelHospital'),
                            school: sourceAvailable('mbzMainPanelSchool'),
                            educationInsight: sourceAvailable('mbzMainPanelEducationInsight')
                        };

                        if (!status.startupCommerce) {
                            hideTab('mbzMainPanelStartupCommerce');
                        }
                        if (!status.overview) {
                            hideTab('mbzMainPanelOverview');
                        }
                        if (!status.rank) {
                            hideTab('mbzMainPanelRank');
                        }
                        if (!status.hr) {
                            hideTab('mbzMainPanelHr');
                        }
                        if (!status.neighbor) {
                            hideTab('mbzMainPanelNeighbor');
                        }
                        if (!status.mfdsRisk) hideTab('mbzMainPanelMfdsRisk');
                        if (!status.construction) {
                            hideTab('mbzMainPanelConstruction');
                        }
                        if (!status.hospital) {
                            hideTab('mbzMainPanelHospital');
                        }
                        if (!status.school) {
                            hideTab('mbzMainPanelSchool');
                        }
                        if (!status.educationInsight) {
                            hideTab('mbzMainPanelEducationInsight');
                        }

                        renumberTabs();

                        hub.querySelectorAll('.mbz-main-tab-btn').forEach(function (btn) {
                            btn.addEventListener('click', function () {
                                activateTab(this.getAttribute('data-target'));
                            });
                        });

                        var defaultPanel = hub.getAttribute('data-default-panel') || 'mbzMainPanelOverview';
                        var initialBtn = hub.querySelector('.mbz-main-tab-btn[data-target="' + defaultPanel + '"]') || hub.querySelector('.mbz-main-tab-btn');

                        if (initialBtn) {
                            activateTab(initialBtn.getAttribute('data-target'));
                        }
                    }

                    if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
                        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', initMarketbizDetailTabs);
                    } else {
                        initMarketbizDetailTabs();
                    }
                })();
            </script>
            <!-- 분석 탐색 탭 끝 -->
            </div>
            <!-- /#mbzDetailTabAnchor -->

            

            <!-- MBZ R52: keep the product-compare stylesheet outside the movable source stash.
                 Moving a <link rel=stylesheet> node during tab mounting makes browsers refetch it
                 when cache is disabled; this preserves identical styling/cascade while avoiding
                 the duplicate request. -->
            <link rel="stylesheet" type="text/css"
                  href="/type/common/css/company-detail-product-compare-r43.css?ver=20261010195546&amp;mbzperf=20260831-r43" />
            <div id="mbzProductSectionSource" class="mbz-source-stash">
                <hr class="page-break">

                
                    <div class="grid-title mbz-panel-title" id="mbzSectionProduct">
                        <div class="title">기업 제품 정보</div>
                    </div>
                    <div class="mbz-qk-anchor mbz-quick-menu" data-major-word="제품" data-qk-short="제품정보"
                         data-activate-target="mbzMainPanelOverview">제품 정보
                    </div>
                    <div>
                        <table class="mbz-table">
                            <colgroup>
                                <col width="15%" class="mbz-col1">
                                <col width="45%">
                                <col width="15%">
                                <col width="25%">
                            </colgroup>
                            <tbody>
                            
    <!-- 이 fragment 전용 스타일 -->
    <!-- R43: product-compare static CSS externalized; callers load the asset only when the product section exists. -->

    <tr class="mbz-tr mbz-product-similar-row">
        <td class="mbz-td font08em mbz-product-similar-cell" colspan="4">
            <div class="mbz-product-similar-inline-head">
                <span class="mbz-product-similar-title">제품이 비슷한 기업</span>
                <div id="similar-firm-summary" class="font10em dark"></div>
            </div>
            
            <!-- 비동기 로딩 영역 -->
            <div id="similar-firm-container">
                <div class="similar-loading-text">
                    <span class="loading-spinner"></span>
                    <span>제품 유사 기업 분석 중...</span>
                </div>
            </div>
        </td>
    </tr>

    
        <script>
            $(document).ready(function() {

                '<div>(제품 임베딩·공통 키워드 기준)</div>'
                + '<div class="font09em middle-gray">※ 제품 설명/키워드를 마켓비즈가 숫자화 하여 가장 비슷한 기업 정보를 제공합니다.</div>'
              );
              $('#similar-firm-container').html(
                `<div class="mbz-product-similar-sample-wrap">
                  <picture>
                    <source type="image/webp" media="(max-width: 767.98px)" srcset="/type/common/img/detail/similar_product_02.webp">
                    <source type="image/webp" media="(min-width: 768px)" srcset="/type/common/img/detail/similar_product_01.webp">
                    <source media="(max-width: 767.98px)" srcset="/type/common/img/detail/similar_product_02.png">
                    <source media="(min-width: 768px)" srcset="/type/common/img/detail/similar_product_01.png">
                    <img src="/type/common/img/detail/similar_product_01.png"
                         alt="유사 제품 포트폴리오 이미지"
                         style="width:100%; height:auto; image-rendering:auto;"
                         loading="lazy" decoding="async">
                  </picture>

                  <div class="mbz-sample-overlay mbz-product-similar-overlay">
                    <em class="strong hands italic only-pay-service mbz-wave-cta is-premium no-title"
                        data-id="AS003"
                        title="프리미엄 구독 시 제품이 비슷한 기업과 제품 포트폴리오 비교를 확인할 수 있습니다.">
                      제품유사기업 프리미엄 구독하기
                    </em>
                  </div>
                </div>`
            );
            });
        </script>
    

    

    

                            <tr class="mbz-tr" id="major-product-list" style="justify-content: unset;">

            src="/type/common/js/product-report-launcher.js?ver=20261010195546&amp;prux=20260830-r72"></script>

                   data-col-name="esnt_hndl_item_nm" data-change-val="" title="변경 직전 값 보기"></i>

        <!-- 무료/비회원/스탠다드 등 샘플이 필요한 권한에서만 노출 -->

        </button>
    </div>

                                </th>

                                <td class="mbz-td font08em" colspan="3">
                                    <div class="mbz-product-sample-preview mbz-free-product-report-entry  is-guest">
                                        <div class="mbz-free-product-report-card is-guest">
                                            <div class="mbz-free-product-report-copy">
                                                <span class="mbz-free-product-report-kicker">PRODUCT MARKET REPORT</span>
                                                <strong>이 기업은 제품시장 보고서를 제공합니다.</strong>
                                                <p>보고서 가능 제품은 <b>1</b>개입니다. 비회원 화면에서는 제품명을 공개하지 않으며, 로그인 또는 무료가입 후 주요제품과 구매 가능한 제품보고서를 확인할 수 있습니다.</p>

                                                        class="mbz-access-cta mbz-access-cta--free mbz-free-view-btn"
                                                        onclick="return fn_joinFreeMajorProduct(this);"
                                                        aria-label="로그인 또는 무료회원 가입 후 주요제품 보기">
                                                    <span>로그인·무료가입 후 보기</span><span class="mbz-free-view-arrow" aria-hidden="true">›</span>
                                                </button>
                                            </div>
                                        </div>

                                });
                            </script>

            </div>

            <div id="mbzRankSectionSource" class="mbz-source-stash" data-rank-data-state="LOCKED_AVAILABLE">
                <!-- 기업 상대위치 시작: 실제 데이터 존재/권한/오류 상태를 분리한다. -->
                <div class="mbz-companyscale-index-container mbz-rank-section  mbz-rank-section--preview">
                    
                    
                        <section class="mbz-rank-sample-shell" data-rank-preview data-nosnippet aria-labelledby="mbzRankPreviewTitle">
    <div class="grid-title mbz-panel-title">
        <div class="title" id="mbzRankPreviewTitle">기업 상대위치</div>
    </div>

    <div class="mbz-rank-sample-preview mbz-sample-watermark">
        <div class="mbz-rank-sample-visual" aria-hidden="true">
            <div class="mbz-rank-sample-hero">
                <div class="mbz-rank-sample-hero-main mbz-rank-sample-copy">
                    <span>기업 상대위치</span>
                    <strong>비교범위별 상대위치를 한눈에 확인합니다.</strong>
                    <em>지표별 모집단을 기준으로 상대위치와 비교구간을 제공합니다.</em>
                    <div class="mbz-rank-sample-chip-row"><i>최상위</i><i>상위</i><i>중위</i><i>하위</i></div>
                </div>
                <div class="mbz-rank-sample-policy mbz-rank-sample-copy"><i>권한별 지표</i><i>동점 공동순위</i><i>모집단 기준</i></div>
            </div>
            <div class="mbz-rank-sample-tabs mbz-rank-sample-copy">
                <i><b>종합규모</b><span>상위 31%</span></i>
                <i><b>국내 평균급여</b><span>상위 42%</span></i>
                <i><b>국내 1인매출</b><span>상위 37%</span></i>
                
            </div>
            <div class="mbz-rank-sample-body">
                <div class="mbz-rank-sample-gauge"><span></span><i></i><b class="mbz-rank-sample-gauge-value">상위 31%</b></div>
                <div class="mbz-rank-sample-scopes mbz-rank-sample-copy">
                    <i><b>전체 비교기업</b><span>상위 31%</span></i>
                    <i><b>산업 비교</b><span>상위 35%</span></i>
                    <i><b>기업유형 비교</b><span>상위 29%</span></i>
                    <i><b>지역 비교</b><span>상위 33%</span></i>
                </div>
            </div>
            <div class="mbz-rank-sample-foot mbz-rank-sample-copy"><i>자동 해석 SAMPLE</i><i>데이터 기준 SAMPLE</i></div>
        </div>

        <div class="mbz-rank-sample-overlay">
            <div class="mbz-industry-sample-lock-card mbz-rank-sample-lock-card">
                <span>샘플 미리보기</span>
                <strong>기업 상대위치는 스탠다드부터 확인할 수 있습니다.</strong>
                
                <p>프리미엄에서는 현재 기업에서 실제 산출 가능한 국내 고용·보상·생산성 상대위치를 확장해 확인합니다.</p>

                    </colgroup>
                    <tbody>
                    
                        <tr class="mbz-tab-wrop-tr">
                            <th class="mbz-tab-wrop-th">홈페이지<i style="display: none;" class="fa fa-comment mbz-icon"
                                                               data-col-name="" data-change-val=""
                                                               title="변경 직전 값 보기"></i></th>
                            <td class="mbz-tab-wrop-td strong italic" colspan="3"><button type='button' class='mbz-access-cta mbz-access-cta--standard mbz-field-access-btn only-pay-service' data-id='AS002' aria-label='스탠다드에서 홈페이지 보기'><span class='fa fa-lock mbz-field-access-btn__icon' aria-hidden='true'></span><span>스탠다드에서 보기</span></button></td>
                            
                        </tr>
                    
                    
                    
                        <script>
                            function fn_contactAddInfoPop(bno) {

                                if (ajaxStatus && ajaxStatus.readyState != 4) {
                                    ajaxStatus.abort();
                                }

                                // 새로운 AJAX 요청 시작
                                mbzCheckSession2({//동기식으로 세션체크하면서 로딩이미지 띄움.
                                    type: "POST",
                                    dataType: "JSON",
                                    url: "/getContactAddInfoList",
                                    data: JSON.stringify({bno: bno}),
                                    contentType: "application/json;charset=utf-8",
                                    timeout: 15000,
                                    beforeSend: function (xhr) {
                                        xhr.setRequestHeader('X-CSRF-TOKEN', $('input[name="_csrf"]').val());
                                    },
                                    complete: function () {
                                        $(".loading-wrap-02").hide();
                                    },
                                    error: function (xhr) {
                                        // 에러 발생 시 모달에 에러 메시지 표시
                                        //console.log(xhr);
                                        fn_alertModalOpen(xhr); // 모달 표시
                                    },
                                    success: function (response) {
                                        //console.log(response);
                                        if (response.resultCode === "000") {
                                            const dataList = response.contactAddList;

                                            viewContactAllInfo(dataList, bno);

                                            fn_custModalOpen('idContactAddModal');

                                        } else {
                                            fn_alertModalOpen(response); // 모달 표시
                                        }
                                    }
                                });
                            }

                            function viewContactAllInfo(dataList, bno) {

                                const tableBody = document.getElementById("contractAddTableBody");
                                /* $(".linkBno").data("bno", bno); */
                                const _bno = fn_util_addBizNoDelimeter(bno);

                                $(".copyBno").data("bno", _bno);

                                contactAddLabelCoNm.innerHTML = ''; // 기존 내용 초기화
                                tableBody.innerHTML = ''; // 기존 내용 초기화

                                contactAddLabelCoNm.innerText = ((window.mbzCompanyClientMeta && window.mbzCompanyClientMeta.companyName) || '') + ' / ' + _bno;

                                dataList.forEach(item => {
                                    const row = document.createElement("tr");
                                    row.className = "mbz-pop-tr"; // 클래스 추가
                                    const regDate = fn_util_addDateDelimeter(item.regDate);
                                    row.innerHTML = `
															<th class="mbz-pop-td">${item.cmnNm}</th>
															<td class="mbz-pop-td">${item.ifNmSource}</td>
															<td class="mbz-pop-td">${item.contCtn}</td>
															<td class="mbz-pop-td">${regDate}</td>
													`;
                                    tableBody.appendChild(row);
                                });
                            }
                        </script>

                        <tr class="mbz-tab-wrop-tr">

                            </td>

                            <span style="float: right;">

                                                        aria-hidden='true' data-bs-toggle='tooltip' data-html='true'
                                                        data-is-auto-show="false">주소정보 갱신</button>
                                            </span>
                        </td>

            <script language="javascript">
                $(document).ready(function () {
                    $('.pop-update-address').click(function () {
                        $.ajax({
                            url: "/mbz/api/time/available",
                            method: "GET",
                            dataType: "json",
                            success: function (data) {
                                if (data.available) {
                                    alert('주소 변경을 위해 확인 절차가 필요합니다\n회원가입 후 진행해주세요');
                                    fn_joinFree();
                                } else {
                                    alert(data.message || "이용 불가 시간입니다.");
                                }
                            },
                            error: function (xhr) {
                                console.error(xhr);
                                alert("시간 확인 중 오류가 발생했습니다.");
                            }
                        });
                    });
                });
            </script>

            <div class="mbz-contact-privacy-note" role="note">
                ※개인사업자는 대표자명/연락처/주소 개인정보처리방침을 준수합니다.
            </div>


            <script>
                $(document).ready(function () {
                    // ccngHisList 데이터 (여기서는 주석을 문자열로 직접 사용)
                    var ccngHisDataString = (window.mbzCompanyClientMeta && window.mbzCompanyClientMeta.changeHistoryJson) || '';
                    if (!ccngHisDataString) return;

                    // HTML 속성에서 읽은 문자열은 브라우저가 이미 엔티티를 복원한다.
                    // 레거시 엔티티가 남은 데이터만 한 번 더 정규화한다.
                    var jsonString = String(ccngHisDataString)
                        .replace(/&quot;/g, '"')
                        .replace(/&lt;/g, '<')
                        .replace(/&gt;/g, '>')
                        .replace(/&#39;/g, "'");

                    var arrayStart = jsonString.indexOf('[');
                    var arrayEnd = jsonString.lastIndexOf(']');
                    if (arrayStart < 0 || arrayEnd < arrayStart) {
                        console.warn('[CompanyChangeHistory] array payload not found');
                        return;
                    }

                    jsonString = jsonString.substring(arrayStart, arrayEnd + 1)
                        .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
                        .trim();

                    var ccngHisData = [];
                    try {
                        var parsedChangeHistory = JSON.parse(jsonString);
                        if (!Array.isArray(parsedChangeHistory)) {
                            console.warn('[CompanyChangeHistory] payload is not an array');
                            return;
                        }
                        ccngHisData = parsedChangeHistory;
                    } catch (e) {
                        console.warn('[CompanyChangeHistory] JSON parse skipped', e);
                        return;
                    }


                    // ccngHisList가 비어있지 않은지 확인
                    if (ccngHisData.length === 0) {
                        //console.error("ccngHisData가 비어 있습니다.");
                    } else {
                        // ccngHisData를 루프
                        ccngHisData.forEach(function (item) {
                            //console.log(`data-col-name= ${item.engColNm}, data-change-val= ${item.prevColVal}`);
                            //console.log(item); // 각 객체 출력
                            // 추가 로직은 여기서 수행
                            var colName = item.eng_col_nm; // 데이터에서 컬럼 이름 가져오기
                            var nowVal = item.col_val; // 데이터에서 이전 값 가져오기
                            var prevVal = item.prev_col_val; // 데이터에서 이전 값 가져오기

                            // 해당하는 data-col-name을 가진 요소 찾기
                            $('i[data-col-name="' + colName + '"]').each(function () {
                                if (colName == "esnt_hndl_item_nm" && prevVal != "") {
                                    $('.more-link').trigger('click');
                                    $(this).attr('data-change-val', prevVal); // data-change-val 속성 설정
                                } else if (colName == "bdge_hash_tokens" && (nowVal != "" || prevVal != "")) {
                                    $(this).attr('data-change-val', nowVal + prevVal); // data-change-val 속성 설정
                                } else {
                                    $(this).attr('data-change-val', prevVal); // data-change-val 속성 설정
                                }
                            });
                        });
                    }

                    doCcngHis();
                });

                function doCcngHis() {
                    document.querySelectorAll('.mbz-icon').forEach(icon => {
                        // data-change-val이 비어있는 경우 아이콘 숨기기
                        if (icon.getAttribute('data-change-val') && icon.getAttribute('data-change-val') != '') {
                            icon.style.display = '';
                        }

                        icon.addEventListener('click', function () {
                            const thElement = this.closest('th');
                            const thIndex = Array.from(thElement.parentNode.children).indexOf(thElement);

                            const currentRow = this.closest('tr');
                            const tds = currentRow.querySelectorAll('td');

                            // td의 인덱스가 th의 인덱스와 일치해야 하므로 thIndex에 +1
                            const adjustedIndex = thIndex > 0 ? thIndex - 1 : thIndex;

                            if (tds.length > adjustedIndex && adjustedIndex >= 0) {
                                const targetTd = tds[adjustedIndex];
                                const currentValue = targetTd.innerHTML;

                                const previousValue = this.getAttribute('data-change-val');

                                if (this.dataset.clicked !== 'true') {
                                    // 현재값을 data-now-val에 저장
                                    this.setAttribute('data-now-val', currentValue);

                                    // 이전 값으로 변경
                                    targetTd.classList.add('crossed');
                                    targetTd.innerHTML = `<span><span class="font09em italic strong" style="color: #dc3545;">변경직전값 〉 ${previousValue}</span></span>`;
                                    this.classList.add('hovered'); // hover 색상 유지

                                    // 5초 후 원래 값으로 복원
                                    const timeoutId = setTimeout(() => {
                                        targetTd.classList.remove('crossed');
                                        targetTd.innerHTML = currentValue; // 현재값으로 복원
                                        this.classList.remove('hovered'); // 원래 색으로 돌아가기
                                        this.dataset.clicked = 'false'; // 클릭 상태 업데이트
                                    }, 5000);

                                    this.dataset.clicked = 'true'; // 클릭 상태 업데이트
                                    this.dataset.timeoutId = timeoutId; // 타임아웃 ID 저장
                                } else {
                                    // 재클릭 시 data-now-val에서 현재값 복원
                                    const storedCurrentValue = this.getAttribute('data-now-val');
                                    clearTimeout(this.dataset.timeoutId); // 기존 타임아웃 취소
                                    targetTd.classList.remove('crossed');
                                    targetTd.innerHTML = storedCurrentValue; // 현재값으로 복원
                                    this.classList.remove('hovered'); // 원래 색으로 돌아가기
                                    this.dataset.clicked = 'false'; // 클릭 상태 초기화
                                }
                            } else {
                                console.error('유효하지 않은 td 인덱스입니다. 인덱스:', adjustedIndex, 'td 개수:', tds.length);
                            }
                        });
                    });
                }
            </script>
            <!-- 새로운 테이블디자인적용 끝 -->
