                <script>
                (function () {
                    function setFinanceTabNoScroll(idx) {
                        var wrap = document.getElementById('mbzFinanceTabList');
                        var source = document.getElementById('mbzFinanceInsightSource');
                        var tab = document.getElementById('tabBtn' + idx);
                        var panel = document.getElementById('tabcont' + idx);

                        if (!wrap || !source || !tab || !panel) return;

                        /* 활성 표시는 AJAX보다 먼저, 재무·인증 영역 안에서만 처리한다. */
                        wrap.querySelectorAll('.tabBtn').forEach(function (el) {
                            var active = el === tab;
                            el.classList.toggle('active', active);
                            el.setAttribute('aria-selected', active ? 'true' : 'false');
                        });

                        source.querySelectorAll('.ibContArea').forEach(function (p) {
                            var active = p === panel;
                            p.classList.toggle('show', active);
                            p.classList.toggle('active', active);
                            p.style.display = active ? 'block' : 'none';
                            p.setAttribute('aria-hidden', active ? 'false' : 'true');
                        });

                        /* 먼저 한 프레임을 그린 뒤 무거운 패널 이벤트를 전달한다. */
                        if (typeof window.mbzDispatchCompanyDetailTabActivated === 'function') {
                            window.requestAnimationFrame(function () {
                                window.setTimeout(function () {
                                    window.mbzDispatchCompanyDetailTabActivated(
                                        idx,
                                        tab,
                                        panel,
                                        'finance-tab-controller'
                                    );
                                }, 0);
                            });
                        }
                    }

                    function getFinanceTabMeta() {
                        var wrap = document.getElementById('mbzFinanceTabList');
                        return {
                            hasFullFinance: !!wrap && wrap.getAttribute('data-has-full-finance') === 'true',
                            hasMajorFinance: !!wrap && wrap.getAttribute('data-has-major-finance') === 'true',
                            hasAuthHistory: !!wrap && wrap.getAttribute('data-has-auth-history') === 'true',
                            hasCeoHistory: !!wrap && wrap.getAttribute('data-has-ceo-history') === 'true',
                            hasEmployeeHistory: !!wrap && wrap.getAttribute('data-has-employee-history') === 'true'
                        };
                    }

                    function shouldOpenInsightTab() {
                        var shouldOpenInsight = false;

                        try {
                            shouldOpenInsight = sessionStorage.getItem('financeInsightSubTab') === '1';
                            if (shouldOpenInsight) {
                                sessionStorage.removeItem('financeInsightSubTab');
                            }
                        } catch (e) {}

                        if (!shouldOpenInsight) {
                            shouldOpenInsight =
                                window.location.hash === '#tabcont8' ||
                                window.location.hash === '#finance-insight';
                        }

                        return shouldOpenInsight;
                    }

                    // 요구 우선순위:
                    // 재무제표(2) > 주요재무제표(9) > 인증/허가(1) > 대표자변경이력(3) > 종업원수변경추이(4)
                    // 재무인사이트(8)는 sessionStorage/hash로 명시 진입했을 때만 우선
                    function pickInitialFinanceTab() {
                        var meta = getFinanceTabMeta();

                        if (shouldOpenInsightTab()) return '8';
                        if (meta.hasMajorFinance) return '9';
                        if (meta.hasFullFinance) return '2';
                        if (meta.hasAuthHistory) return '1';
                        if (meta.hasCeoHistory) return '3';
                        if (meta.hasEmployeeHistory) return '4';

                        // 전부 0건이어도 최소한 비어있는 기본 탭 하나는 열어 준다.
                        return '2';
                    }

                    function hasVisibleFinancePanel() {
                        var activeTab = document.querySelector('#mbzFinanceTabList .tabBtn.active');
                        if (!activeTab) return false;

                        var seq = activeTab.getAttribute('data-seq');
                        var panel = document.getElementById('tabcont' + seq);
                        if (!panel) return false;

                        return window.getComputedStyle(panel).display !== 'none';
                    }

                    function initFinanceTabState(force) {
                        if (!force && hasVisibleFinancePanel()) return;
                        setFinanceTabNoScroll(pickInitialFinanceTab());
                    }

                    function bindFinanceTabEvents() {
                        var wrap = document.getElementById('mbzFinanceTabList');
                        if (!wrap) return;
                        if (wrap.dataset.bound === 'true') return;

                        wrap.dataset.bound = 'true';

                        wrap.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
                            var btn = e.target.closest('.tabBtn');
                            if (!btn || !wrap.contains(btn)) return;

                            e.preventDefault();

                            var seq = btn.getAttribute('data-seq');
                            if (!seq) return;

                            setFinanceTabNoScroll(seq);
                            // 무료페이지는 tabBtn5의 inline onclick(getRelationCoDataList(this))는 그대로 둔다.
                            // 여기서는 패널 전환만 담당한다.
                        });
                    }

                    var mbzQuickMenuInitScheduled = false;

                    function initMarketbizQuickMenuPage() {
                        if (mbzQuickMenuInitScheduled || typeof window.initMbzQuickMenu !== 'function') {
                            return;
                        }

                        mbzQuickMenuInitScheduled = true;

                        var mobile = window.matchMedia && window.matchMedia('(max-width: 1024px)').matches;
                        if (!mobile) {
                            window.requestAnimationFrame(function () { window.initMbzQuickMenu(); });
                            return;
                        }

                        /*
                         * 모바일에서는 FAB 셸만 즉시 만든다.
                         * 전체 문서 anchor 스캔은 사용자가 FAB를 눌렀을 때 1회 실행한다.
                         * 로딩 후 지연 작업으로 본문 스크롤을 끊지 않는다.
                         */
                        window.initMbzQuickMenu({ shellOnly: true });
                    }

                    function initMarketbizDetailPage(force) {
                        bindFinanceTabEvents();
                        initFinanceTabState(!!force);
                        initMarketbizQuickMenuPage();
                    }

                    if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
                        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {
                            initMarketbizDetailPage(true);
                        });
                    } else {
                        initMarketbizDetailPage(true);
                    }

                    window.addEventListener('load', function () {
                        initMarketbizDetailPage(false);
                    });

                    window.addEventListener('pageshow', function () {
                        initMarketbizDetailPage(false);
                    });
                })();
                </script>

    <!-- R39: auth-history styles moved to company-detail-finance-panels-r39.css -->

         tabindex="-1" data-auth-loaded="true" data-auth-ajax-enabled="false" data-auth-count="0" data-auth-bno="6168628898">

        <!--

         * 실제 인증·허가 목록은 조회하지 않고, count > 0이면 정적 STANDARD/PREMIUM 샘플을 즉시 렌더링한다.
         * Standard/Premium만 탭 활성화 시 실제 패널을 AJAX로 지연 조회한다.

        <!--

         * Thymeleaf fragment replacement가 조건보다 먼저 처리되면 유료회원에서도
         * 무료 샘플 panel과 AJAX placeholder가 동시에 렌더링되어 동일 id가 중복될 수 있다.
         -->
        

        
    </div>

                <div id="tabcont2" class="ibContArea" role="tabpanel" aria-labelledby="tabBtn2" tabindex="-1">

        <script>
            function fn_joinFree() {
                if (typeof window.mbzOpenServiceAuthGate === 'function') {
                    return window.mbzOpenServiceAuthGate({reason: 'FREE_ACCESS', serviceLabel: '기업상세 무료 기능'});
                }
                if (typeof window.fn_login === 'function') return window.fn_login({reason: 'FREE_ACCESS'});
                location.href = '/loginPage?authReason=FREE_ACCESS&redirectUrl=' + encodeURIComponent(location.pathname + location.search);
                return false;
            }

            function fn_joinFreeMajorProduct(sourceElement) {
                var redirectUrl = location.pathname + location.search + '#major-product-list';
                if (typeof window.mbzOpenServiceAuthGate === 'function') {
                    return window.mbzOpenServiceAuthGate({
                        reason: 'FREE_ACCESS',
                        serviceLabel: '주요제품',
                        redirectUrl: redirectUrl,
                        sourceElement: sourceElement
                    });
                }
                if (typeof window.fn_login === 'function') {
                    return window.fn_login({
                        reason: 'FREE_ACCESS',
                        serviceLabel: '주요제품',
                        redirectUrl: redirectUrl
                    });
                }
                location.href = '/loginPage?authReason=FREE_ACCESS&serviceLabel=' + encodeURIComponent('주요제품')
                    + '&redirectUrl=' + encodeURIComponent(redirectUrl);
                return false;
            }
        </script>

<script>
    function getRelationCoDataList(obj) {
        var crno = String($(obj).data("crno"));

        if ($("#relationCompanyList").hasClass("loaded")) return;

        var relationCount = parseInt($("#relationCoCount").val() || "0", 10);
        if (isNaN(relationCount)) relationCount = 0;

        if (nvl(crno) === "" || relationCount === 0) {
            $(".relationCoListCount").html("(0)");
            $("#relationCompanyList").html(
                `<tr class="mbz-tab-tr">
                    <td class="mbz-tab-td" colspan="6">조회 된 내용이 없습니다.</td>
                </tr>`
            );
            $("#relationCompanyList").addClass("loaded");
            return;
        }

        var relationCountText = relationCount > 0 ? "1+" : "0";
        $(".relationCoListCount").html("(" + relationCountText + ")");
        $("#relationCompanyList").html(
            `<tr class="mbz-tab-tr">
                <td class="mbz-tab-td red strong italic" colspan="6">
                    관계사 현황은 무료에서 건수만 제공합니다.
                </td>
            </tr>`
        );
        $("#relationCompanyList").addClass("loaded");
    }
</script>

		<footer><style>
/* 공통 스타일 */
.btn-page-move {
    position: fixed;
    right: 10px;
    background-color: #fd7e14;
    color: #fff !important;
    -webkit-text-fill-color: #fff !important;
    border: none;
    border-radius: 50%;
    width: 50px;
    height: 50px;
    font-size: 20px;
    font-weight: bold;
    cursor: pointer;
    box-shadow: 0 4px 8px rgba(0,0,0,0.2);
    z-index: 10000;
    opacity: 0;
    visibility: hidden;
    transform: translateY(6px);
    transition: opacity 0.3s ease, transform 0.3s ease, visibility 0.3s ease;

    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: center;
    gap: 1px;
}

/* 맨 위 / 맨 아래 위치만 분리 */
.btn-top-move {
    bottom: 90px;
}

/*
 * R3-B.8G: 다크모드에서도 페이지 이동 버튼의 동작 상태를 색으로 오해하지 않도록
 * 맨 위/맨 아래 버튼을 동일한 MarketBZ orange action color로 유지한다.
 * contents.css의 과거 dark top-button gray 규칙보다 이 공통 footer 계약을 우선한다.
 */
body.darkmode .btn-top-move,
html[data-theme="dark"] body .btn-top-move,
body[data-theme="dark"] .btn-top-move {
    background-color: #fd7e14 !important;
}

.btn-bottom-move {
    bottom: 35px;
}

/* 기업상세 전용: 데이터 오류/정정 요청 */
.btn-report-move {
    bottom: 165px;
    background:
        radial-gradient(circle at 50% 24%, rgba(255,255,255,.28) 0 10%, transparent 28%),
        linear-gradient(180deg, #ff6b57 0%, #ff4d3a 28%, #e0281c 68%, #b81510 100%);
    color: #fff;
    border: none;
    box-shadow:
        0 10px 24px rgba(122, 8, 8, .38),
        0 0 20px rgba(255, 70, 58, .28);
    overflow: visible;
    animation: mbzReportGlow 2.2s ease-in-out infinite;
}

.btn-report-move::before {
    content: "";
    position: absolute;
    inset: -8px;
    border-radius: 50%;
    background:
        conic-gradient(
            from 0deg,
            transparent 0deg,
            rgba(255, 245, 210, .0) 18deg,
            rgba(255, 244, 210, .92) 38deg,
            rgba(255, 120, 80, .55) 58deg,
            transparent 78deg,
            transparent 180deg,
            rgba(255, 244, 210, .78) 215deg,
            rgba(255, 110, 70, .42) 238deg,
            transparent 262deg,
            transparent 360deg
        );
    filter: blur(1px);
    opacity: .88;
    z-index: -1;
    animation: mbzReportSweep 2.8s ease-in-out infinite;
}

.btn-report-move::after {
    content: "";
    position: absolute;
    inset: -12px;
    border-radius: 50%;
    background: radial-gradient(circle, rgba(255, 80, 65, .22) 0%, rgba(255, 80, 65, .08) 45%, transparent 72%);
    z-index: -2;
    animation: mbzReportAura 2.2s ease-in-out infinite;
}

.btn-report-move .mbz-report-svg {
    width: 23px;
    height: 23px;
    color: #fff;
    stroke: currentColor;
    stroke-width: 2.3;
    fill: none;
    filter:
        drop-shadow(0 1px 2px rgba(0,0,0,.25))
        drop-shadow(0 0 6px rgba(255,255,255,.18));
    animation: mbzReportIconBlink 1.8s ease-in-out infinite;
}

.btn-report-move .mbz-siren-ray,
.btn-report-move .mbz-report-spark {
    transform-origin: center;
    animation: mbzReportRayBlink 1.8s ease-in-out infinite;
}

.btn-report-move .mbz-report-spark,
.btn-report-move .mbz-siren-ray {
    transform-origin: center;
    animation: mbzReportSirenRay .72s ease-in-out infinite alternate;
}

.btn-report-move .btn-page-move-font {
    color: #fff;
    font-weight: 800;
    letter-spacing: -0.02em;
    text-shadow: 0 1px 2px rgba(0,0,0,.22);
}

.btn-report-move:hover {
    transform: translateY(-2px) scale(1.06);
    box-shadow:
        0 14px 28px rgba(122, 8, 8, .44),
        0 0 34px rgba(255, 82, 64, .46);
}

@keyframes mbzReportSweep {
    0% {
        transform: rotate(-8deg) scale(.96);
        opacity: .42;
    }
    25% {
        transform: rotate(8deg) scale(1.01);
        opacity: .95;
    }
    50% {
        transform: rotate(18deg) scale(1.02);
        opacity: .52;
    }
    75% {
        transform: rotate(42deg) scale(1.01);
        opacity: .90;
    }
    100% {
        transform: rotate(58deg) scale(.96);
        opacity: .40;
    }
}

@keyframes mbzReportAura {
    0%, 100% {
        transform: scale(.92);
        opacity: .42;
    }
    50% {
        transform: scale(1.14);
        opacity: .82;
    }
}

@keyframes mbzReportGlow {
    0%, 100% {
        box-shadow:
            0 10px 24px rgba(122, 8, 8, .34),
            0 0 16px rgba(255, 70, 58, .18);
    }
    50% {
        box-shadow:
            0 12px 28px rgba(122, 8, 8, .42),
            0 0 32px rgba(255, 82, 64, .42);
    }
}

@keyframes mbzReportIconBlink {
    0%, 100% {
        opacity: .9;
        transform: scale(1);
    }
    50% {
        opacity: 1;
        transform: scale(1.06);
    }
}

@keyframes mbzReportRayBlink {
    0%, 100% {
        opacity: .36;
    }
    50% {
        opacity: 1;
    }
}

/* 나타날 때 효과 */
.btn-page-move.show {
    opacity: 1;
    visibility: visible;
    transform: translateY(0);
}

/* 호버 효과 */
.btn-page-move:hover {
    transform: scale(1.1);
}

.mbz-page-arrow-svg {
    display: block;
    width: 20px;
    height: 20px;
    color: #fff !important;
    stroke: currentColor;
    stroke-width: 2.8;
    stroke-linecap: round;
    stroke-linejoin: round;
    fill: none;
    pointer-events: none;
}

.btn-page-move-font {
    font-size: 0.5em;
    color: white;
    line-height: 1;
    font-weight: 700;
    white-space: nowrap;
}

/* 모바일 대응 */
@media (max-width: 1025px) {
    .btn-page-move {
        right: 5px;
        width: 35px;
        height: 35px;
        font-size: 14px;
    }

    .btn-top-move {
        bottom: 90px;
    }

    .btn-bottom-move {
        bottom: 50px;
    }

    .btn-page-move-font {
        display: none;
    }

    .btn-report-move {
        bottom: 145px;
    }
}
</style>
<button id="companyReportButton"
        class="btn-page-move btn-report-move"
        type="button"
        title="데이터 오류·정정 요청"
        aria-label="데이터 오류·정정 요청">
    <svg class="mbz-report-svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true">
        <path class="mbz-siren-ray" d="M12 2.8v2.1"/>
        <path class="mbz-siren-ray" d="M5 5.6l1.6 1.3"/>
        <path class="mbz-siren-ray" d="M19 5.6l-1.6 1.3"/>
        <path d="M8.4 11.2c0-2 1.6-3.8 3.6-3.8s3.6 1.8 3.6 3.8v4.3H8.4z"/>
        <path d="M7.3 15.8h9.4"/>
        <path d="M6.2 18.7h11.6"/>
        <circle cx="12" cy="11.2" r="1.15" />
    </svg>
    <div class="btn-page-move-font">정정요청</div>
</button>
<!-- Top / Bottom Button -->
<button id="topButton" class="btn-page-move btn-top-move" type="button" title="맨 위로" aria-label="맨 위로">
    <svg class="mbz-page-arrow-svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false">
        <path d="M6 15l6-6 6 6"/>
    </svg>
    <div class="btn-page-move-font">맨위로</div>
</button>

<button id="bottomButton" class="btn-page-move btn-bottom-move" type="button" title="맨 아래로" aria-label="맨 아래로">
    <svg class="mbz-page-arrow-svg" viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false">
        <path d="M6 9l6 6 6-6"/>
    </svg>
    <div class="btn-page-move-font">맨아래</div>
</button>

<script>
(function () {
    const topButton = document.getElementById("topButton");
    const bottomButton = document.getElementById("bottomButton");
    const reportButton = document.getElementById("companyReportButton");

    if (!topButton || !bottomButton) {
        return;
    }

    const detailMatched = (window.location.pathname || "").match(/^\/companyDetail\/([0-9]{10})$/i);
    const isCompanyDetail = !!detailMatched;
    const currentBno = detailMatched ? detailMatched[1] : "";

    topButton.dataset.bound = "Y";
    bottomButton.dataset.bound = "Y";

    if (reportButton) {
        reportButton.dataset.bound = "Y";

        if (!isCompanyDetail) {
            reportButton.remove();
        } else {
            reportButton.classList.add("show");
        }
    }

    function textOf(selector) {
        const el = document.querySelector(selector);
        return el && el.textContent ? el.textContent.replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim() : "";
    }

    function getCurrentCompanyName() {
        return textOf(".company-detail.companyname")
            || textOf(".search-list-group.companyname")
            || textOf(".coNm")
            || "";
    }

    function openCompanyDataReportPopup() {
        if (!isCompanyDetail) {
            return false;
        }

        const qs = new URLSearchParams();
        qs.set("bNo", currentBno);
        qs.set("coNm", getCurrentCompanyName());
        qs.set("pageUrl", window.location.href);
        qs.set("_ts", String(Date.now()));

        const src = "/mail/contact/report?" + qs.toString();

        if (window.MBZCommonPopup && typeof window.MBZCommonPopup.buildFramePopup === "function") {
            window.MBZCommonPopup.buildFramePopup(
                "mbzCompanyDataReportWrap",
                "데이터 오류·정정 요청",
                src,
                "920px",
                "min(650px, calc(100vh - 32px))"
            );
            return false;
        }

        $("<iframe id=\"iframeCompanyDataReportPop\" class=\"pop_iframe on\" src=\"" + src + "\" frameborder=\"0\" " +
            "style=\"left:0; top:0; width:100%; height:100vh; position:fixed; z-index:999999; border:0; backdrop-filter:blur(3px);\" />")
            .appendTo("body");

        if (window.MBZScrollLock) {
            window.MBZScrollLock.lock();
        } else {
            $("body").css("overflow-y", "hidden");
        }

        return false;
    }

    function syncPageMoveButtons() {
        const scrollTop = window.scrollY || document.documentElement.scrollTop || 0;
        const maxScroll = Math.max(
            0,
            document.documentElement.scrollHeight - window.innerHeight
        );

        if (scrollTop > 200) {
            topButton.classList.add("show");
        } else {
            topButton.classList.remove("show");
        }

        if ((maxScroll - scrollTop) > 200) {
            bottomButton.classList.add("show");
        } else {
            bottomButton.classList.remove("show");
        }

        if (reportButton && isCompanyDetail) {
            reportButton.classList.add("show");
        }
    }

    if (reportButton && isCompanyDetail) {
        reportButton.addEventListener("click", openCompanyDataReportPopup);
    }

    topButton.addEventListener("click", function () {
        window.scrollTo({
            top: 0,
            behavior: "smooth"
        });
    });

    bottomButton.addEventListener("click", function () {
        window.scrollTo({
            top: document.documentElement.scrollHeight,
            behavior: "smooth"
        });
    });

    window.addEventListener("scroll", syncPageMoveButtons, { passive: true });
    window.addEventListener("resize", syncPageMoveButtons);

    syncPageMoveButtons();
})();
</script>
